{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018bXRd3DXPbMadMiLM4DKHm","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# StellarStage Carnival – Clean Architecture Blueprint\n     2→\n     3→_This document is a living, executable specification of how StellarStage Carnival’s JavaScript / TypeScript code-base is organised.  All code blocks are copy-paste ready for production and every design decision is mapped to a concrete implementation detail._\n     4→\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→## 1. Layered Onion\n     8→\n     9→```mermaid\n    10→graph LR\n    11→    A[Presentation<br/>React + Three.js] --> B[Application<br/>GraphQL / WebSocket Gateway]\n    12→    B --> C[Use-Cases<br/>Orchestrators]\n    13→    C --> D[Domain<br/>Entities & Value Objects]\n    14→    C --> E[Domain Services<br/>Factories, Strategies, State-Machines]\n    15→    C --> F[Event Bus<br/>Observer Pattern]\n    16→    C --> G[Port Adapters<br/>Ethereum, IPFS, Streaming]\n    17→    G --> H[External Systems<br/>L1/L2, CDN, Storage]\n    18→```\n    19→\n    20→* Nothing in `Domain` depends on outer layers.  \n    21→* `Use-Cases` know only the `Domain` and well-defined `Ports`.  \n    22→* `Adapters` implement the `Ports`, allowing for painless chain upgrades.\n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## 2. Domain Layer (Pure, No Side-Effects)\n    27→\n    28→### 2.1 Value Objects\n    29→\n    30→```ts\n    31→// src/domain/value-objects/Address.ts\n    32→export class Address {\n    33→  constructor(private readonly value: string) {\n    34→    if (!/^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/.test(value)) {\n    35→      throw new Error(`Invalid Ethereum address ${value}`);\n    36→    }\n    37→  }\n    38→  equals(other: Address): boolean {\n    39→    return this.value.toLowerCase() === other.value.toLowerCase();\n    40→  }\n    41→  toString(): string {\n    42→    return this.value;\n    43→  }\n    44→}\n    45→```\n    46→\n    47→### 2.2 Entities\n    48→\n    49→```ts\n    50→// src/domain/entities/Show.ts\n    51→import { AggregateRoot } from '../shared/AggregateRoot';\n    52→import { Address } from '../value-objects/Address';\n    53→\n    54→export interface ActProps {\n    55→  id: string;\n    56→  name: string;\n    57→  performerAddr: Address;\n    58→  startAt: Date;\n    59→  endAt: Date;\n    60→}\n    61→\n    62→export interface ShowProps {\n    63→  id: string;\n    64→  title: string;\n    65→  owner: Address;\n    66→  acts: ActProps[];\n    67→}\n    68→\n    69→export class Show extends AggregateRoot<ShowProps> {\n    70→  get id() { return this.props.id; }\n    71→  get acts() { return this.props.acts; }\n    72→\n    73→  addAct(act: ActProps) {\n    74→    if (this.props.acts.find(a => a.id === act.id)) {\n    75→      throw new Error(`Act ${act.id} already exists`);\n    76→    }\n    77→    this.props.acts.push(act);\n    78→    this.addDomainEvent({ type: 'ActAdded', payload: act });\n    79→  }\n    80→}\n    81→```\n    82→\n    83→---\n    84→\n    85→## 3. Application Layer (Use-Cases)\n    86→\n    87→### 3.1 Port Definitions\n    88→\n    89→```ts\n    90→// src/application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway.ts\n    91→import { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\n    92→\n    93→export interface NftGateway {\n    94→  mintPass(pass: Pass, to: string): Promise<string>; // returns tx hash\n    95→  upgradePass(tokenId: string, traits: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;\n    96→}\n    97→```\n    98→\n    99→### 3.2 Use-Case Service Example – MintShowPass\n   100→\n   101→```ts\n   102→// src/application/use-cases/MintShowPass.ts\n   103→import { ShowRepository } from '../ports/repositories/ShowRepository';\n   104→import { PassFactory } from '../services/PassFactory';\n   105→import { NftGateway } from '../ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\n   106→import { EventBus } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n   107→\n   108→export class MintShowPass {\n   109→  constructor(\n   110→    private readonly showRepo: ShowRepository,\n   111→    private readonly passFactory: PassFactory,\n   112→    private readonly nftGateway: NftGateway,\n   113→    private readonly bus: EventBus,\n   114→  ) {}\n   115→\n   116→  async execute(cmd: { showId: string; userWallet: string }) {\n   117→    const show = await this.showRepo.byId(cmd.showId);\n   118→    if (!show) throw new Error('Show not found');\n   119→\n   120→    const pass = this.passFactory.createForShow(show, cmd.userWallet);\n   121→\n   122→    const txHash = await this.nftGateway.mintPass(pass, cmd.userWallet);\n   123→    await this.showRepo.savePass(pass);\n   124→\n   125→    this.bus.publish({ type: 'PassMinted', payload: { txHash, passId: pass.id } });\n   126→    return { txHash, passId: pass.id };\n   127→  }\n   128→}\n   129→```\n   130→\n   131→---\n   132→\n   133→## 4. Domain Services\n   134→\n   135→### 4.1 Factory Pattern – PassFactory\n   136→\n   137→```ts\n   138→// src/application/services/PassFactory.ts\n   139→import { ulid } from 'ulid';\n   140→import { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\n   141→import { Show } from '../../domain/entities/Show';\n   142→\n   143→export class PassFactory {\n   144→  createForShow(show: Show, ownerWallet: string): Pass {\n   145→    return new Pass({\n   146→      id: ulid(),\n   147→      showId: show.id,\n   148→      ownerWallet,\n   149→      level: 1,\n   150→      traits: this.defaultTraits(show),\n   151→    });\n   152→  }\n   153→\n   154→  private defaultTraits(show: Show) {\n   155→    return {\n   156→      theme: show.title.toLowerCase().includes('comedy') ? 'funny' : 'epic',\n   157→      rarityScore: Math.random() * 100,\n   158→    };\n   159→  }\n   160→}\n   161→```\n   162→\n   163→---\n   164→\n   165→## 5. Infrastructure Layer\n   166→\n   167→### 5.1 Ethereum Adapter (uses ethers.js)\n   168→\n   169→```ts\n   170→// src/infrastructure/blockchain/EthersNftGateway.ts\n   171→import { ethers } from 'ethers';\n   172→import { NftGateway } from '../../application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\n   173→import abi from './StellarPass.json';\n   174→\n   175→export class EthersNftGateway implements NftGateway {\n   176→  private contract: ethers.Contract;\n   177→\n   178→  constructor(\n   179→    private readonly provider: ethers.providers.JsonRpcProvider,\n   180→    private readonly signer: ethers.Signer,\n   181→    contractAddress: string,\n   182→  ) {\n   183→    this.contract = new ethers.Contract(contractAddress, abi, signer);\n   184→  }\n   185→\n   186→  async mintPass(pass, to) {\n   187→    const tx = await this.contract.safeMint(to, pass.metadataUri);\n   188→    await tx.wait(2);\n   189→    return tx.hash;\n   190→  }\n   191→\n   192→  async upgradePass(tokenId, traits) {\n   193→    const tx = await this.contract.setTraits(tokenId, JSON.stringify(traits));\n   194→    await tx.wait(1);\n   195→  }\n   196→}\n   197→```\n   198→\n   199→### 5.2 Event Bus Adapter\n   200→\n   201→```ts\n   202→// src/infrastructure/event/WebSocketEventBus.ts\n   203→import WebSocket from 'ws';\n   204→import { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../../application/ports/event/EventBus';\n   205→\n   206→export class WebSocketEventBus implements EventBus {\n   207→  private readonly wss = new WebSocket.Server({ noServer: true });\n   208→  private readonly listeners: Set<(e: DomainEvent) => void> = new Set();\n   209→\n   210→  publish(event: DomainEvent): void {\n   211→    const json = JSON.stringify(event);\n   212→    this.listeners.forEach(cb => cb(event));\n   213→    this.wss.clients.forEach(client => client.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN && client.send(json));\n   214→  }\n   215→\n   216→  subscribe(cb: (e: DomainEvent) => void): void {\n   217→    this.listeners.add(cb);\n   218→  }\n   219→}\n   220→```\n   221→\n   222→---\n   223→\n   224→## 6. Reactive Pipeline (Observer → Strategy → State-Machine)\n   225→\n   226→```ts\n   227→// src/application/services/ShowStateMachine.ts\n   228→import { createMachine, interpret } from 'xstate';\n   229→import { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n   230→\n   231→export class ShowStateMachine {\n   232→  private readonly service;\n   233→\n   234→  constructor(private bus: EventBus) {\n   235→    const machine = createMachine({\n   236→      id: 'show',\n   237→      initial: 'scheduled',\n   238→      states: {\n   239→        scheduled: { on: { ActStarted: 'live' } },\n   240→        live: { on: { ActEnded: 'break' } },\n   241→        break: { on: { ActStarted: 'live', ShowEnded: 'ended' } },\n   242→        ended: { type: 'final' },\n   243→      },\n   244→    });\n   245→\n   246→    this.service = interpret(machine).onTransition(state => {\n   247→      bus.publish({ type: 'ShowStateChanged', payload: { state: state.value } });\n   248→    });\n   249→    this.bus.subscribe(this.onEvent.bind(this));\n   250→  }\n   251→\n   252→  start() { this.service.start(); }\n   253→\n   254→  private onEvent(evt: DomainEvent) {\n   255→    this.service.send(evt.type as any);\n   256→  }\n   257→}\n   258→```\n   259→\n   260→---\n   261→\n   262→## 7. Proxy Smart Contracts (Upgrade-safe)\n   263→\n   264→```solidity\n   265→// contracts/StellarPassProxy.sol (snippet)\n   266→contract StellarPassProxy is ERC1967Proxy {\n   267→    constructor(address _logic, bytes memory _data)\n   268→        ERC1967Proxy(_logic, _data) {}\n   269→}\n   270→```\n   271→\n   272→_Key points_\n   273→\n   274→1. Implementation logic lives in `StellarPass.sol`.\n   275→2. Proxy delegates calls, preserving storage layout for future upgrades.\n   276→3. Royalties enforced via EIP-2981 in implementation contract.\n   277→\n   278→---\n   279→\n   280→## 8. Persistence & CQRS\n   281→\n   282→* Command side uses a fast Hot-Path DB (PostgreSQL + Prisma).\n   283→* Query side is materialised into Apollo GraphQL subgraphs.  \n   284→* Both are kept in sync via the Event Bus to ensure eventual consistency.\n   285→\n   286→---\n   287→\n   288→## 9. Observability & Error Handling\n   289→\n   290→```ts\n   291→// src/infrastructure/logging/pinoLogger.ts\n   292→import pino from 'pino';\n   293→export const logger = pino({ level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? 'info' });\n   294→\n   295→// Example usage\n   296→try {\n   297→  await nftGateway.mintPass(pass, wallet);\n   298→} catch (err) {\n   299→  logger.error({ err, passId: pass.id }, 'Failed to mint pass');\n   300→  throw new MintPassError(pass.id, err);\n   301→}\n   302→```\n   303→\n   304→---\n   305→\n   306→## 10. Testing Strategy\n   307→\n   308→1. Domain & Use-Cases – pure unit tests (Jest).  \n   309→2. Contract tests for Ports (pact-style mocks).  \n   310→3. Solidity contracts – Foundry + Hardhat coverage > 95 %.  \n   311→4. All code blocks are copy-paste ready for production and every design decision is mapped to a concrete implementation detail._\n\n---\n\n## 1. Layered Onion\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    A[Presentation<br/>React + Three.js] --> B[Application<br/>GraphQL / WebSocket Gateway]\n    B --> C[Use-Cases<br/>Orchestrators]\n    C --> D[Domain<br/>Entities & Value Objects]\n    C --> E[Domain Services<br/>Factories, Strategies, State-Machines]\n    C --> F[Event Bus<br/>Observer Pattern]\n    C --> G[Port Adapters<br/>Ethereum, IPFS, Streaming]\n    G --> H[External Systems<br/>L1/L2, CDN, Storage]\n```\n\n* Nothing in `Domain` depends on outer layers.  \n* `Use-Cases` know only the `Domain` and well-defined `Ports`.  \n* `Adapters` implement the `Ports`, allowing for painless chain upgrades.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Domain Layer (Pure, No Side-Effects)\n\n### 2.1 Value Objects\n\n```ts\n// src/domain/value-objects/Address.ts\nexport class Address {\n  constructor(private readonly value: string) {\n    if (!/^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/.test(value)) {\n      throw new Error(`Invalid Ethereum address ${value}`);\n    }\n  }\n  equals(other: Address): boolean {\n    return this.value.toLowerCase() === other.value.toLowerCase();\n  }\n  toString(): string {\n    return this.value;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 2.2 Entities\n\n```ts\n// src/domain/entities/Show.ts\nimport { AggregateRoot } from '../shared/AggregateRoot';\nimport { Address } from '../value-objects/Address';\n\nexport interface ActProps {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  performerAddr: Address;\n  startAt: Date;\n  endAt: Date;\n}\n\nexport interface ShowProps {\n  id: string;\n  title: string;\n  owner: Address;\n  acts: ActProps[];\n}\n\nexport class Show extends AggregateRoot<ShowProps> {\n  get id() { return this.props.id; }\n  get acts() { return this.props.acts; }\n\n  addAct(act: ActProps) {\n    if (this.props.acts.find(a => a.id === act.id)) {\n      throw new Error(`Act ${act.id} already exists`);\n    }\n    this.props.acts.push(act);\n    this.addDomainEvent({ type: 'ActAdded', payload: act });\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Application Layer (Use-Cases)\n\n### 3.1 Port Definitions\n\n```ts\n// src/application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway.ts\nimport { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\n\nexport interface NftGateway {\n  mintPass(pass: Pass, to: string): Promise<string>; // returns tx hash\n  upgradePass(tokenId: string, traits: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 Use-Case Service Example – MintShowPass\n\n```ts\n// src/application/use-cases/MintShowPass.ts\nimport { ShowRepository } from '../ports/repositories/ShowRepository';\nimport { PassFactory } from '../services/PassFactory';\nimport { NftGateway } from '../ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\nimport { EventBus } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n\nexport class MintShowPass {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly showRepo: ShowRepository,\n    private readonly passFactory: PassFactory,\n    private readonly nftGateway: NftGateway,\n    private readonly bus: EventBus,\n  ) {}\n\n  async execute(cmd: { showId: string; userWallet: string }) {\n    const show = await this.showRepo.byId(cmd.showId);\n    if (!show) throw new Error('Show not found');\n\n    const pass = this.passFactory.createForShow(show, cmd.userWallet);\n\n    const txHash = await this.nftGateway.mintPass(pass, cmd.userWallet);\n    await this.showRepo.savePass(pass);\n\n    this.bus.publish({ type: 'PassMinted', payload: { txHash, passId: pass.id } });\n    return { txHash, passId: pass.id };\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Domain Services\n\n### 4.1 Factory Pattern – PassFactory\n\n```ts\n// src/application/services/PassFactory.ts\nimport { ulid } from 'ulid';\nimport { Pass } from '../../domain/entities/Pass';\nimport { Show } from '../../domain/entities/Show';\n\nexport class PassFactory {\n  createForShow(show: Show, ownerWallet: string): Pass {\n    return new Pass({\n      id: ulid(),\n      showId: show.id,\n      ownerWallet,\n      level: 1,\n      traits: this.defaultTraits(show),\n    });\n  }\n\n  private defaultTraits(show: Show) {\n    return {\n      theme: show.title.toLowerCase().includes('comedy') ? 'funny' : 'epic',\n      rarityScore: Math.random() * 100,\n    };\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Infrastructure Layer\n\n### 5.1 Ethereum Adapter (uses ethers.js)\n\n```ts\n// src/infrastructure/blockchain/EthersNftGateway.ts\nimport { ethers } from 'ethers';\nimport { NftGateway } from '../../application/ports/blockchain/NftGateway';\nimport abi from './StellarPass.json';\n\nexport class EthersNftGateway implements NftGateway {\n  private contract: ethers.Contract;\n\n  constructor(\n    private readonly provider: ethers.providers.JsonRpcProvider,\n    private readonly signer: ethers.Signer,\n    contractAddress: string,\n  ) {\n    this.contract = new ethers.Contract(contractAddress, abi, signer);\n  }\n\n  async mintPass(pass, to) {\n    const tx = await this.contract.safeMint(to, pass.metadataUri);\n    await tx.wait(2);\n    return tx.hash;\n  }\n\n  async upgradePass(tokenId, traits) {\n    const tx = await this.contract.setTraits(tokenId, JSON.stringify(traits));\n    await tx.wait(1);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 Event Bus Adapter\n\n```ts\n// src/infrastructure/event/WebSocketEventBus.ts\nimport WebSocket from 'ws';\nimport { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../../application/ports/event/EventBus';\n\nexport class WebSocketEventBus implements EventBus {\n  private readonly wss = new WebSocket.Server({ noServer: true });\n  private readonly listeners: Set<(e: DomainEvent) => void> = new Set();\n\n  publish(event: DomainEvent): void {\n    const json = JSON.stringify(event);\n    this.listeners.forEach(cb => cb(event));\n    this.wss.clients.forEach(client => client.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN && client.send(json));\n  }\n\n  subscribe(cb: (e: DomainEvent) => void): void {\n    this.listeners.add(cb);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Reactive Pipeline (Observer → Strategy → State-Machine)\n\n```ts\n// src/application/services/ShowStateMachine.ts\nimport { createMachine, interpret } from 'xstate';\nimport { EventBus, DomainEvent } from '../ports/event/EventBus';\n\nexport class ShowStateMachine {\n  private readonly service;\n\n  constructor(private bus: EventBus) {\n    const machine = createMachine({\n      id: 'show',\n      initial: 'scheduled',\n      states: {\n        scheduled: { on: { ActStarted: 'live' } },\n        live: { on: { ActEnded: 'break' } },\n        break: { on: { ActStarted: 'live', ShowEnded: 'ended' } },\n        ended: { type: 'final' },\n      },\n    });\n\n    this.service = interpret(machine).onTransition(state => {\n      bus.publish({ type: 'ShowStateChanged', payload: { state: state.value } });\n    });\n    this.bus.subscribe(this.onEvent.bind(this));\n  }\n\n  start() { this.service.start(); }\n\n  private onEvent(evt: DomainEvent) {\n    this.service.send(evt.type as any);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Proxy Smart Contracts (Upgrade-safe)\n\n```solidity\n// contracts/StellarPassProxy.sol (snippet)\ncontract StellarPassProxy is ERC1967Proxy {\n    constructor(address _logic, bytes memory _data)\n        ERC1967Proxy(_logic, _data) {}\n}\n```\n\n_Key points_\n\n1. Implementation logic lives in `StellarPass.sol`.\n2. Proxy delegates calls, preserving storage layout for future upgrades.\n3. Royalties enforced via EIP-2981 in implementation contract.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Persistence & CQRS\n\n* Command side uses a fast Hot-Path DB (PostgreSQL + Prisma).\n* Query side is materialised into Apollo GraphQL subgraphs.  \n* Both are kept in sync via the Event Bus to ensure eventual consistency.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Observability & Error Handling\n\n```ts\n// src/infrastructure/logging/pinoLogger.ts\nimport pino from 'pino';\nexport const logger = pino({ level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? 'info' });\n\n// Example usage\ntry {\n  await nftGateway.mintPass(pass, wallet);\n} catch (err) {\n  logger.error({ err, passId: pass.id }, 'Failed to mint pass');\n  throw new MintPassError(pass.id, err);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0182KfASVB2XDdmgtNGqMbzP","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# StellarStage Carnival – Smart-Contract Upgradeability Guide\n     3→> File: `docs/contracts/UPGRADEABILITY_GUIDE.md`  \n     4→> Last updated: 2024-05-29\n     5→\n     6→StellarStage Carnival’s live-show economy is expected to evolve rapidly—new gamification rules, ticketing mechanics, and royalty streams will be introduced every season. To support this pace **without forcing holders to burn & remint their NFTs**, every on-chain component is designed to be _upgradeable_ via the **Transparent Proxy pattern** (OpenZeppelin’s implementation) and protected by rigorous governance and testing gates.\n     7→\n     8→This document is the single source of truth for:\n     9→* Contract layout & storage guidelines  \n    10→* Safe upgrade workflow (local → testnet → mainnet)  \n    11→* Governance & emergency protocols  \n    12→* Hardhat + TypeChain scripts and examples  \n    13→\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## 1. Architectural Overview\n    17→\n    18→```mermaid\n    19→flowchart LR\n    20→    subgraph Proxy Layer\n    21→        Proxy(Transparent\\nProxy)\n    22→    end\n    23→    subgraph Logic Layer\n    24→        V1(ShowPassV1.sol)\n    25→        V2(ShowPassV2.sol)\n    26→    end\n    27→    Proxy --delegatecall--> V1\n    28→    Proxy --upgradeTo--> V2\n    29→```\n    30→\n    31→* **Proxy Layer** (immutable): Stores state; delegates calls to the active logic contract.  \n    32→* **Logic Layer** (upgradeable): ShowPassV1, V2, etc. Houses business rules only.  \n    33→* **Admin Layer** (governed by `CarnivalGovernor`): Owns the proxy and controls `upgradeTo`.\n    34→\n    35→> NOTE: Upgrades never migrate data—the `Proxy` keeps the same storage slots across versions.\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## 2. Directory Conventions\n    40→\n    41→```\n    42→contracts/\n    43→  proxy/\n    44→    BaseProxy.sol\n    45→  pass/\n    46→    implementations/\n    47→      ShowPassV1.sol\n    48→      ShowPassV2.sol\n    49→    facets/          # For Diamond-style experiments\n    50→scripts/\n    51→  upgrades/\n    52→    propose-upgrade.ts\n    53→    approve-upgrade.ts\n    54→    execute-upgrade.ts\n    55→```\n    56→\n    57→---\n    58→\n    59→## 3. Writing an Upgradeable Contract\n    60→\n    61→### 3.1. Solidity Rules of Thumb\n    62→1. **No constructors** – use `initialize()` instead.  \n    63→2. **Never use `selfdestruct`** – breaks the proxy.  \n    64→3. **Constants/APIs** – can be added, **never removed or reordered**.  \n    65→4. **Storage gap** – reserve `uint256[50] private __gap;` for future vars.\n    66→\n    67→### 3.2. Example – `ShowPassV1.sol`\n    68→\n    69→```solidity\n    70→// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n    71→pragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n    72→\n    73→import \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/token/ERC721/extensions/ERC721URIStorageUpgradeable.sol\";\n    74→import \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/utils/UUPSUpgradeable.sol\";\n    75→import \"../proxy/BaseProxy.sol\";\n    76→\n    77→contract ShowPassV1 is ERC721URIStorageUpgradeable, UUPSUpgradeable {\n    78→    /* ============ State ============ */\n    79→    mapping(uint256 => uint256) private _exp; // Fan XP per pass\n    80→\n    81→    /* ============ Initializer ============ */\n    82→    function initialize(string memory name_, string memory symbol_) public initializer {\n    83→        __ERC721_init(name_, symbol_);\n    84→        __UUPSUpgradeable_init();\n    85→    }\n    86→\n    87→    /* ============ Core Logic ============ */\n    88→    function gainExp(uint256 tokenId, uint256 amt) external {\n    89→        require(_exists(tokenId), \"PASS_NOT_FOUND\");\n    90→        _exp[tokenId] += amt;\n    91→    }\n    92→\n    93→    /* ============ Upgradability ============ */\n    94→    function _authorizeUpgrade(address newImpl) internal override onlyProxyAdmin {}\n    95→    \n    96→    uint256[49] private __gap; // 49 because we used one slot for _exp\n    97→}\n    98→```\n    99→\n   100→---\n   101→\n   102→## 4. Storage Layout & Compatibility\n   103→\n   104→Run `npx hardhat storage:layout` before and after every change.  \n   105→Breaking changes (detected via `@openzeppelin/upgrades-core`) will **halt the CI**.\n   106→\n   107→Common pitfalls:\n   108→* Re-ordering state variables  \n   109→* Changing inheritances that inject storage (`Ownable`, `Pausable`)  \n   110→* Converting `uint8` → `uint256` (size mismatch)\n   111→\n   112→---\n   113→\n   114→## 5. Upgrade Workflow\n   115→\n   116→1. **Develop** new implementation in a feature branch.  \n   117→2. **Unit & drive tests** (`pnpm test:contracts`).  \n   118→3. **Propose upgrade** (generates calldata & JSON diff):\n   119→\n   120→```bash\n   121→pnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/propose-upgrade.ts \\\n   122→  --proxy 0xProxyAddress \\\n   123→  --impl  contracts/pass/implementations/ShowPassV2.sol\n   124→```\n   125→\n   126→4. **Governor review** – core devs & community vote on chain.  \n   127→5. **Execute upgrade** (TimeLock enforced):\n   128→\n   129→```bash\n   130→pnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/execute-upgrade.ts --proposal 123\n   131→```\n   132→\n   133→6. **Post-upgrade smoke test**: read `version()` & run snapshot tests.\n   134→\n   135→---\n   136→\n   137→## 6. Governance & Emergency\n   138→\n   139→* `CarnivalGovernor` (ERC-5805) owns the proxy admin role.  \n   140→* Emergency multisig (5/9) can trigger `_rollback()` if:\n   141→  * Severe bug endangering funds\n   142→  * DAO vote unreachable within 12h\n   143→\n   144→---\n   145→\n   146→## 7. Hardhat Helper Scripts\n   147→\n   148→### 7.1. `propose-upgrade.ts`\n   149→\n   150→```ts\n   151→import { ethers, upgrades } from \"hardhat\";\n   152→import fs from \"node:fs/promises\";\n   153→\n   154→async function main() {\n   155→  const proxy = \"0xProxyAddress\";\n   156→  const NewImpl = await ethers.getContractFactory(\"ShowPassV2\");\n   157→  const proposal = await upgrades.prepareUpgrade(proxy, NewImpl);\n   158→  await fs.writeFile(\n   159→    `./scripts/upgrades/proposals/${Date.now()}.json`,\n   160→    JSON.stringify({ proxy, proposal }, null, 2)\n   161→  );\n   162→  console.log(\"Upgrade prepared at:\", proposal);\n   163→}\n   164→\n   165→main().catch((e) => {\n   166→  console.error(e);\n   167→  process.exit(1);\n   168→});\n   169→```\n   170→\n   171→### 7.2. `execute-upgrade.ts`\n   172→\n   173→```ts\n   174→import { ethers, upgrades } from \"hardhat\";\n   175→import proposal from \"./proposals/1685381110.json\";\n   176→\n   177→async function main() {\n   178→  const admin = await ethers.getSigner(\"upgrade-admin\");\n   179→  await upgrades.upgradeProxy(proposal.proxy, proposal.proposal, { signer: admin });\n   180→  console.log(\"Proxy upgraded!\");\n   181→}\n   182→\n   183→main().catch((e) => {\n   184→  console.error(e);\n   185→  process.exit(1);\n   186→});\n   187→```\n   188→\n   189→---\n   190→\n   191→## 8. Test Matrix\n   192→\n   193→| Layer | Tooling                     | Responsibility                       |\n   194→|-------|-----------------------------|--------------------------------------|\n   195→| Unit  | Hardhat + viem             | Business invariants (XP, rarity)     |\n   196→| Integration | Anvil fork + Foundry | Cross-contract interactions          |\n   197→| E2E   | Hardhat Network + Mocha    | Upgrade, rollback, governance flow   |\n   198→\n   199→CI runs on every push; mainnet deploy uses the same tagged Docker image.\n   200→\n   201→---\n   202→\n   203→## 9. FAQ\n   204→\n   205→**Q: Can we migrate to a Diamond (EIP-2535) later?**  \n   206→A: Yes—the proxy can delegate to a DiamondCutFacet in V3. Storage layout care is mandatory.\n   207→\n   208→**Q: What if an upgrade fails mid-transaction?**  \n   209→A: The upgrade is atomic; either `upgradeTo` succeeds or the proxy keeps the old impl.\n   210→\n   211→---\n   212→\n   213→## 10. To support this pace **without forcing holders to burn & remint their NFTs**, every on-chain component is designed to be _upgradeable_ via the **Transparent Proxy pattern** (OpenZeppelin’s implementation) and protected by rigorous governance and testing gates.\n\nThis document is the single source of truth for:\n* Contract layout & storage guidelines  \n* Safe upgrade workflow (local → testnet → mainnet)  \n* Governance & emergency protocols  \n* Hardhat + TypeChain scripts and examples  \n\n---\n\n## 1. Architectural Overview\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Proxy Layer\n        Proxy(Transparent\\nProxy)\n    end\n    subgraph Logic Layer\n        V1(ShowPassV1.sol)\n        V2(ShowPassV2.sol)\n    end\n    Proxy --delegatecall--> V1\n    Proxy --upgradeTo--> V2\n```\n\n* **Proxy Layer** (immutable): Stores state; delegates calls to the active logic contract.  \n* **Logic Layer** (upgradeable): ShowPassV1, V2, etc. Houses business rules only.  \n* **Admin Layer** (governed by `CarnivalGovernor`): Owns the proxy and controls `upgradeTo`.\n\n> NOTE: Upgrades never migrate data—the `Proxy` keeps the same storage slots across versions.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Directory Conventions\n\n```\ncontracts/\n  proxy/\n    BaseProxy.sol\n  pass/\n    implementations/\n      ShowPassV1.sol\n      ShowPassV2.sol\n    facets/          # For Diamond-style experiments\nscripts/\n  upgrades/\n    propose-upgrade.ts\n    approve-upgrade.ts\n    execute-upgrade.ts\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Writing an Upgradeable Contract\n\n### 3.1. Solidity Rules of Thumb\n1. **No constructors** – use `initialize()` instead.  \n2. **Never use `selfdestruct`** – breaks the proxy.  \n3. **Constants/APIs** – can be added, **never removed or reordered**.  \n4. **Storage gap** – reserve `uint256[50] private __gap;` for future vars.\n\n### 3.2. Example – `ShowPassV1.sol`\n\n```solidity\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/token/ERC721/extensions/ERC721URIStorageUpgradeable.sol\";\nimport \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/utils/UUPSUpgradeable.sol\";\nimport \"../proxy/BaseProxy.sol\";\n\ncontract ShowPassV1 is ERC721URIStorageUpgradeable, UUPSUpgradeable {\n    /* ============ State ============ */\n    mapping(uint256 => uint256) private _exp; // Fan XP per pass\n\n    /* ============ Initializer ============ */\n    function initialize(string memory name_, string memory symbol_) public initializer {\n        __ERC721_init(name_, symbol_);\n        __UUPSUpgradeable_init();\n    }\n\n    /* ============ Core Logic ============ */\n    function gainExp(uint256 tokenId, uint256 amt) external {\n        require(_exists(tokenId), \"PASS_NOT_FOUND\");\n        _exp[tokenId] += amt;\n    }\n\n    /* ============ Upgradability ============ */\n    function _authorizeUpgrade(address newImpl) internal override onlyProxyAdmin {}\n    \n    uint256[49] private __gap; // 49 because we used one slot for _exp\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Storage Layout & Compatibility\n\nRun `npx hardhat storage:layout` before and after every change.  \nBreaking changes (detected via `@openzeppelin/upgrades-core`) will **halt the CI**.\n\nCommon pitfalls:\n* Re-ordering state variables  \n* Changing inheritances that inject storage (`Ownable`, `Pausable`)  \n* Converting `uint8` → `uint256` (size mismatch)\n\n---\n\n## 5. Upgrade Workflow\n\n1. **Develop** new implementation in a feature branch.  \n2. **Unit & drive tests** (`pnpm test:contracts`).  \n3. **Propose upgrade** (generates calldata & JSON diff):\n\n```bash\npnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/propose-upgrade.ts \\\n  --proxy 0xProxyAddress \\\n  --impl  contracts/pass/implementations/ShowPassV2.sol\n```\n\n4. **Governor review** – core devs & community vote on chain.  \n5. **Execute upgrade** (TimeLock enforced):\n\n```bash\npnpm ts-node scripts/upgrades/execute-upgrade.ts --proposal 123\n```\n\n6. **Post-upgrade smoke test**: read `version()` & run snapshot tests.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Governance & Emergency\n\n* `CarnivalGovernor` (ERC-5805) owns the proxy admin role.  \n* Emergency multisig (5/9) can trigger `_rollback()` if:\n  * Severe bug endangering funds\n  * DAO vote unreachable within 12h\n\n---\n\n## 7. Hardhat Helper Scripts\n\n### 7.1. `propose-upgrade.ts`\n\n```ts\nimport { ethers, upgrades } from \"hardhat\";\nimport fs from \"node:fs/promises\";\n\nasync function main() {\n  const proxy = \"0xProxyAddress\";\n  const NewImpl = await ethers.getContractFactory(\"ShowPassV2\");\n  const proposal = await upgrades.prepareUpgrade(proxy, NewImpl);\n  await fs.writeFile(\n    `./scripts/upgrades/proposals/${Date.now()}.json`,\n    JSON.stringify({ proxy, proposal }, null, 2)\n  );\n  console.log(\"Upgrade prepared at:\", proposal);\n}\n\nmain().catch((e) => {\n  console.error(e);\n  process.exit(1);\n});\n```\n\n### 7.2. `execute-upgrade.ts`\n\n```ts\nimport { ethers, upgrades } from \"hardhat\";\nimport proposal from \"./proposals/1685381110.json\";\n\nasync function main() {\n  const admin = await ethers.getSigner(\"upgrade-admin\");\n  await upgrades.upgradeProxy(proposal.proxy, proposal.proposal, { signer: admin });\n  console.log(\"Proxy upgraded!\");\n}\n\nmain().catch((e) => {\n  console.error(e);\n  process.exit(1);\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Test Matrix\n\n| Layer | Tooling                     | Responsibility                       |\n|-------|-----------------------------|--------------------------------------|\n| Unit  | Hardhat + viem             | Business invariants (XP, rarity)     |\n| Integration | Anvil fork + Foundry | Cross-contract interactions          |\n| E2E   | Hardhat Network + Mocha    | Upgrade, rollback, governance flow   |\n\nCI runs on every push; mainnet deploy uses the same tagged Docker image.\n\n---\n\n## 9. FAQ\n\n**Q: Can we migrate to a Diamond (EIP-2535) later?**  \nA: Yes—the proxy can delegate to a DiamondCutFacet in V3. Storage layout care is mandatory.\n\n**Q: What if an upgrade fails mid-transaction?**  \nA: The upgrade is atomic; either `upgradeTo` succeeds or the proxy keeps the old impl.\n\n---\n\n## 10. Appendix – Sample Rollback Script\n\n```ts\n// rollback.ts\nimport { ethers } from \"hardhat\";\nimport { TransparentUpgradeableProxy__factory } from \"../typechain\";\n\nasync function main() {\n  const proxyAddr = process.argv[2];\n  const prevImpl  = process.argv[3];\n  if (!proxyAddr || !prevImpl) throw new Error(\"Usage: rollback <proxy> <prevImpl>\");\n\n  const admin = await ethers.getSigner(\"multisig-emergency\");\n  const proxy = TransparentUpgradeableProxy__factory.connect(proxyAddr, admin);\n\n  const tx = await proxy.upgradeTo(prevImpl);\n  await tx.wait();\n  console.log(`Rolled back ${proxyAddr} to ${prevImpl}`);\n}\n\nmain().catch(console.error);\n```\n\n---\n\nHappy building, and may your stages remain stellar!  \n– _Carnival Core Devs_\n```","numLines":240,"startLine":1,"totalLines":240}}}

Bootstrapping a Provider & Signer\n    52→```ts\n    53→// src/lib/blockchain.ts\n    54→import { JsonRpcProvider, Wallet } from 'ethers';\n    55→import { config } from 'dotenv';\n    56→import { z } from 'zod';\n    57→\n    58→config();\n    59→\n    60→const EnvSchema = z.object({\n    61→  RPC_URL: z.string().url(),\n    62→  PRIVATE_KEY: z.string().regex(/^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$/),\n    63→  CHAIN_ID: z.coerce.number().positive()\n    64→});\n    65→\n    66→const { RPC_URL, PRIVATE_KEY, CHAIN_ID } = EnvSchema.parse(process.env);\n    67→\n    68→export const provider = new JsonRpcProvider(RPC_URL, CHAIN_ID);\n    69→\n    70→// A signer is _only_ required for state-changing transactions.\n    71→export const signer = new Wallet(PRIVATE_KEY, provider);\n    72→```\n    73→\n    74→---\n    75→\n    76→## 4. Read-Only Queries\n    77→```ts\n    78→// src/queries/isPassUpgradable.ts\n    79→import { Contract } from 'ethers';\n    80→import { provider } from '@/lib/blockchain';\n    81→import ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n    82→\n    83→const SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n    84→\n    85→/**\n    86→ * Check whether a given tokenId can still evolve.\n    87→ */\n    88→export async function isPassUpgradable(tokenId: bigint): Promise<boolean> {\n    89→  const contract = new Contract(SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS, ShowPassABI, provider);\n    90→  try {\n    91→    const level = await contract.currentLevel(tokenId);\n    92→    const maxLevel = await contract.MAX_LEVEL();\n    93→    return level < maxLevel;\n    94→  } catch (error) {\n    95→    console.error('Read error in isPassUpgradable ➜', error);\n    96→    return false;\n    97→  }\n    98→}\n    99→```\n   100→\n   101→---\n   102→\n   103→## 5. Stateful Transactions\n   104→### 5.1 Mint a ShowPass\n   105→```ts\n   106→// src/tx/mintShowPass.ts\n   107→import { Contract } from 'ethers';\n   108→import { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\n   109→import ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n   110→\n   111→const SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n   112→\n   113→/**\n   114→ * Mints a new ShowPass for the connected wallet.\n   115→ * @param tier 0 = GA, 1 = VIP, 2 = Ultra\n   116→ * @param referralCode Optional bytes32 affiliate code\n   117→ */\n   118→export async function mintShowPass(tier: number, referralCode = '0x') {\n   119→  const showPass = new Contract(SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS, ShowPassABI, signer);\n   120→\n   121→  // Optional: Estimate for better UX\n   122→  const gas = await showPass.mint.estimateGas(signer.address, tier, referralCode);\n   123→\n   124→  const tx = await showPass.mint(signer.address, tier, referralCode, {\n   125→    gasLimit: gas + (gas / 10n) // add 10% safety margin\n   126→  });\n   127→\n   128→  console.info(`Mint submitted ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n   129→  const receipt = await tx.wait();\n   130→  console.info(`Mint confirmed in block ${receipt.blockNumber}`);\n   131→  return receipt;\n   132→}\n   133→```\n   134→\n   135→### 5.2 Stake Pass (Voting Escrow Pattern)\n   136→```ts\n   137→// src/tx/stakePass.ts\n   138→import { Contract } from 'ethers';\n   139→import { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\n   140→import VotingEscrowABI from '@/contracts/abi/VotingEscrow.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n   141→import ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n   142→\n   143→const ESCROW = '0xEscR…0LL';\n   144→const SHOW_PASS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n   145→\n   146→export async function stakePass(tokenId: bigint, weeks: number) {\n   147→  const escrow = new Contract(ESCROW, VotingEscrowABI, signer);\n   148→  const pass   = new Contract(SHOW_PASS, ShowPassABI, signer);\n   149→\n   150→  // Approve once, stake many\n   151→  const approved = await pass.isApprovedForAll(signer.address, ESCROW);\n   152→  if (!approved) {\n   153→    const approveTx = await pass.setApprovalForAll(ESCROW, true);\n   154→    await approveTx.wait();\n   155→  }\n   156→\n   157→  const lockUntil = BigInt(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + weeks * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60);\n   158→\n   159→  const tx = await escrow.lock(tokenId, lockUntil);\n   160→  console.info(`Staking tx ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n   161→  return tx.wait();\n   162→}\n   163→```\n   164→\n   165→### 5.3 Cast a Live Vote\n   166→```ts\n   167→// src/tx/castLiveVote.ts\n   168→import { Contract } from 'ethers';\n   169→import { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\n   170→import GovernorABI from '@/contracts/abi/CarnivalGovernor.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n   171→\n   172→const GOVERNOR = '0xFabC…A11A';\n   173→\n   174→export async function castLiveVote(proposalId: bigint, support: 0 | 1 | 2) {\n   175→  const governor = new Contract(GOVERNOR, GovernorABI, signer);\n   176→\n   177→  const params = await governor.proposalSnapshot(proposalId);\n   178→  if (BigInt(Date.now() / 1000) < params.startTime) {\n   179→    throw new Error('Voting has not started yet.');\n   180→  }\n   181→\n   182→  const tx = await governor.castVote(proposalId, support);\n   183→  console.info(`Vote tx ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n   184→  return tx.wait();\n   185→}\n   186→```\n   187→\n   188→---\n   189→\n   190→## 6. Event Subscriptions\n   191→```ts\n   192→// src/subscriptions/showPassEvents.ts\n   193→import { Contract } from 'ethers';\n   194→import { provider } from '@/lib/blockchain';\n   195→import ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n   196→\n   197→const SHOW_PASS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n   198→\n   199→export function subscribeToPassEvolution(cb: (tokenId: bigint, level: number) => void) {\n   200→  const pass = new Contract(SHOW_PASS, ShowPassABI, provider);\n   201→  pass.on('LevelUp', (tokenId, level, event) => {\n   202→    console.debug('Pass evolved:', { tokenId, level, tx: event.transactionHash });\n   203→    cb(tokenId, Number(level));\n   204→  });\n   205→  return () => pass.removeAllListeners('LevelUp');\n   206→}\n   207→```\n   208→\n   209→---\n   210→\n   211→## 7. Error Handling Patterns\n   212→1. Always wrap read calls inside `try/catch` to avoid crashing renders.\n   213→2. Decode revert reasons for on-chain failures:\n   214→```ts\n   215→import { getRpcError } from 'ethers';\n   216→\n   217→try {\n   218→  await contract.doSomething();\n   219→} catch (e) {\n   220→  const reason = getRpcError(e)?.info?.error?.message ?? 'Unknown error';\n   221→  toast.error(`Tx failed: ${reason}`);\n   222→}\n   223→```\n   224→3. Apply exponential back-off when polling confirmations in flaky networks.\n   225→\n   226→---\n   227→\n   228→## 8. Gas & Fee Optimisations\n   229→• Prefer `staticCall` (aka call-static) to simulate transactions off-chain.  \n   230→• Batch multiple mints with `multicall(aggregate)` via `CarnivalMulticaller`.  \n   231→• Use EIP-1559 params: `maxFeePerGas`, `maxPriorityFeePerGas`.  \n   232→• Monitor `baseFee` with `provider.getFeeData()` and queue non-urgent jobs.\n   233→\n   234→---\n   235→\n   236→## 9. Advanced: Meta-Tx & Permit2\n   237→`ShowPassProxy` implements ERC-2771 recipient; you can pay gas on behalf of users.\n   238→\n   239→```ts\n   240→import { signTypedData } from '@wagmi/core'; // or viem\n   241→// …generate typedData object…\n   242→const signature = await signTypedData({ domain, types, value });\n   243→\n   244→await carnivalForwarder.execute({\n   245→  target: SHOW_PASS,\n   246→  data: pass.interface.encodeFunctionData('levelUp', [tokenId, traitHash]),\n   247→  signature\n   248→});\n   249→```\n   250→\n   251→---\n   252→\n   253→## 10. Reference Utilities\n   254→```ts\n   255→// src/utils/retry.ts\n   256→export async function retry<T>(\n   257→  fn: () => Promise<T>,\n   258→  attempts = 3,\n   259→  delayMs = 1_000\n   260→): Promise<T> {\n   261→  try {\n   262→    return await fn();\n   263→  } catch (err) {\n   264→    if (attempts <= 1) throw err;\n   265→    await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, delayMs));\n   266→    return retry(fn, attempts - 1, delayMs * 2);\n   267→  }\n   268→}\n   269→```\n   270→\n   271→---\n   272→\n   273→Happy building, and see you on the Stellar Stage! 🚀\n   274→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Bootstrapping a Provider & Signer\n```ts\n// src/lib/blockchain.ts\nimport { JsonRpcProvider, Wallet } from 'ethers';\nimport { config } from 'dotenv';\nimport { z } from 'zod';\n\nconfig();\n\nconst EnvSchema = z.object({\n  RPC_URL: z.string().url(),\n  PRIVATE_KEY: z.string().regex(/^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$/),\n  CHAIN_ID: z.coerce.number().positive()\n});\n\nconst { RPC_URL, PRIVATE_KEY, CHAIN_ID } = EnvSchema.parse(process.env);\n\nexport const provider = new JsonRpcProvider(RPC_URL, CHAIN_ID);\n\n// A signer is _only_ required for state-changing transactions.\nexport const signer = new Wallet(PRIVATE_KEY, provider);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Read-Only Queries\n```ts\n// src/queries/isPassUpgradable.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { provider } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n\nconst SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n\n/**\n * Check whether a given tokenId can still evolve.\n */\nexport async function isPassUpgradable(tokenId: bigint): Promise<boolean> {\n  const contract = new Contract(SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS, ShowPassABI, provider);\n  try {\n    const level = await contract.currentLevel(tokenId);\n    const maxLevel = await contract.MAX_LEVEL();\n    return level < maxLevel;\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Read error in isPassUpgradable ➜', error);\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Stateful Transactions\n### 5.1 Mint a ShowPass\n```ts\n// src/tx/mintShowPass.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n\nconst SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n\n/**\n * Mints a new ShowPass for the connected wallet.\n * @param tier 0 = GA, 1 = VIP, 2 = Ultra\n * @param referralCode Optional bytes32 affiliate code\n */\nexport async function mintShowPass(tier: number, referralCode = '0x') {\n  const showPass = new Contract(SHOW_PASS_ADDRESS, ShowPassABI, signer);\n\n  // Optional: Estimate for better UX\n  const gas = await showPass.mint.estimateGas(signer.address, tier, referralCode);\n\n  const tx = await showPass.mint(signer.address, tier, referralCode, {\n    gasLimit: gas + (gas / 10n) // add 10% safety margin\n  });\n\n  console.info(`Mint submitted ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n  const receipt = await tx.wait();\n  console.info(`Mint confirmed in block ${receipt.blockNumber}`);\n  return receipt;\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 Stake Pass (Voting Escrow Pattern)\n```ts\n// src/tx/stakePass.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport VotingEscrowABI from '@/contracts/abi/VotingEscrow.json' assert { type: 'json' };\nimport ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n\nconst ESCROW = '0xEscR…0LL';\nconst SHOW_PASS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n\nexport async function stakePass(tokenId: bigint, weeks: number) {\n  const escrow = new Contract(ESCROW, VotingEscrowABI, signer);\n  const pass   = new Contract(SHOW_PASS, ShowPassABI, signer);\n\n  // Approve once, stake many\n  const approved = await pass.isApprovedForAll(signer.address, ESCROW);\n  if (!approved) {\n    const approveTx = await pass.setApprovalForAll(ESCROW, true);\n    await approveTx.wait();\n  }\n\n  const lockUntil = BigInt(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + weeks * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60);\n\n  const tx = await escrow.lock(tokenId, lockUntil);\n  console.info(`Staking tx ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n  return tx.wait();\n}\n```\n\n### 5.3 Cast a Live Vote\n```ts\n// src/tx/castLiveVote.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { signer } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport GovernorABI from '@/contracts/abi/CarnivalGovernor.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n\nconst GOVERNOR = '0xFabC…A11A';\n\nexport async function castLiveVote(proposalId: bigint, support: 0 | 1 | 2) {\n  const governor = new Contract(GOVERNOR, GovernorABI, signer);\n\n  const params = await governor.proposalSnapshot(proposalId);\n  if (BigInt(Date.now() / 1000) < params.startTime) {\n    throw new Error('Voting has not started yet.');\n  }\n\n  const tx = await governor.castVote(proposalId, support);\n  console.info(`Vote tx ➜ ${tx.hash}`);\n  return tx.wait();\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Event Subscriptions\n```ts\n// src/subscriptions/showPassEvents.ts\nimport { Contract } from 'ethers';\nimport { provider } from '@/lib/blockchain';\nimport ShowPassABI from '@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json' assert { type: 'json' };\n\nconst SHOW_PASS = '0x1cA5…BEEF';\n\nexport function subscribeToPassEvolution(cb: (tokenId: bigint, level: number) => void) {\n  const pass = new Contract(SHOW_PASS, ShowPassABI, provider);\n  pass.on('LevelUp', (tokenId, level, event) => {\n    console.debug('Pass evolved:', { tokenId, level, tx: event.transactionHash });\n    cb(tokenId, Number(level));\n  });\n  return () => pass.removeAllListeners('LevelUp');\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Error Handling Patterns\n1. Always wrap read calls inside `try/catch` to avoid crashing renders.\n2. Decode revert reasons for on-chain failures:\n```ts\nimport { getRpcError } from 'ethers';\n\ntry {\n  await contract.doSomething();\n} catch (e) {\n  const reason = getRpcError(e)?.info?.error?.message ?? 'Unknown error';\n  toast.error(`Tx failed: ${reason}`);\n}\n```\n3. Apply exponential back-off when polling confirmations in flaky networks.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Gas & Fee Optimisations\n• Prefer `staticCall` (aka call-static) to simulate transactions off-chain.  \n• Batch multiple mints with `multicall(aggregate)` via `CarnivalMulticaller`.  \n• Use EIP-1559 params: `maxFeePerGas`, `maxPriorityFeePerGas`.  \n• Monitor `baseFee` with `provider.getFeeData()` and queue non-urgent jobs.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Advanced: Meta-Tx & Permit2\n`ShowPassProxy` implements ERC-2771 recipient; you can pay gas on behalf of users.\n\n```ts\nimport { signTypedData } from '@wagmi/core'; // or viem\n// …generate typedData object…\nconst signature = await signTypedData({ domain, types, value });\n\nawait carnivalForwarder.execute({\n  target: SHOW_PASS,\n  data: pass.interface.encodeFunctionData('levelUp', [tokenId, traitHash]),\n  signature\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Reference Utilities\n```ts\n// src/utils/retry.ts\nexport async function retry<T>(\n  fn: () => Promise<T>,\n  attempts = 3,\n  delayMs = 1_000\n): Promise<T> {\n  try {\n    return await fn();\n  } catch (err) {\n    if (attempts <= 1) throw err;\n    await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, delayMs));\n    return retry(fn, attempts - 1, delayMs * 2);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\nHappy building, and see you on the Stellar Stage! 🚀\n```","numLines":274,"startLine":1,"totalLines":274}}}

It lives purely\n     7→ * in the domain layer and knows nothing about how/where it will be persisted\n     8→ * or minted on-chain.  All side-effects are exposed via Domain Events so that\n     9→ * application services and infrastructure adapters can react appropriately.\n    10→ */\n    11→\n    12→import { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';\n    13→\n    14→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    15→/*                              Utility / Helpers                             */\n    16→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    17→\n    18→type EthereumAddress = `0x${string}`;\n    19→\n    20→/**\n    21→ * Very small address validator.  In a real code-base we might depend on\n    22→ * `ethers.utils.isAddress`, but the domain layer avoids heavy libs.\n    23→ */\n    24→function assertValidAddress(address: string): asserts address is EthereumAddress {\n    25→  const re = /^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/;\n    26→  if (!re.test(address)) {\n    27→    throw new DomainError(`Invalid Ethereum address: ${address}`);\n    28→  }\n    29→}\n    30→\n    31→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    32→/*                                  Errors                                    */\n    33→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    34→\n    35→export class DomainError extends Error {\n    36→  readonly name = 'DomainError';\n    37→}\n    38→\n    39→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    40→/*                               Domain Events                                */\n    41→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    42→\n    43→export interface DomainEvent<T = unknown> {\n    44→  readonly name: string;\n    45→  readonly payload: T;\n    46→  readonly occurredOn: Date;\n    47→}\n    48→\n    49→/**\n    50→ * A super-lightweight synchronous event dispatcher used only in the domain\n    51→ * layer to register side-effect-free callbacks. Infrastructure code can wire\n    52→ * this up to a real bus (RabbitMQ, SNS, etc.) at the application boundary.\n    53→ */\n    54→export class DomainEvents {\n    55→  private static handlers: Map<string, Set<(event: DomainEvent) => void>> =\n    56→    new Map();\n    57→\n    58→  static publish(event: DomainEvent): void {\n    59→    const listeners = this.handlers.get(event.name);\n    60→    if (!listeners?.size) return;\n    61→\n    62→    listeners.forEach((cb) => {\n    63→      try {\n    64→        cb(event);\n    65→      } catch (err) {\n    66→        /* eslint-disable no-console */\n    67→        console.error(`[DomainEvents] Handler threw for event ${event.name}`, err);\n    68→        /* eslint-enable  no-console */\n    69→      }\n    70→    });\n    71→  }\n    72→\n    73→  static subscribe(eventName: string, cb: (event: DomainEvent) => void): void {\n    74→    const listeners = this.handlers.get(eventName) ?? new Set();\n    75→    listeners.add(cb);\n    76→    this.handlers.set(eventName, listeners);\n    77→  }\n    78→\n    79→  static clear(): void {\n    80→    this.handlers.clear();\n    81→  }\n    82→}\n    83→\n    84→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    85→/*                               Value Objects                                */\n    86→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    87→\n    88→/** A trait is a mutable property embedded in token metadata. */\n    89→export interface PassTrait {\n    90→  key: string;\n    91→  value: string | number | boolean;\n    92→  /** RFC-3339 ISO string; easier to serialize than Date. */\n    93→  lastUpdated: string;\n    94→}\n    95→\n    96→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    97→/*                                Aggregate                                   */\n    98→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    99→\n   100→interface ShowPassProps {\n   101→  tokenId: bigint; // On-chain tokenId (ERC-721 compatible).\n   102→  showId: string; // UUID of the Show aggregate this pass belongs to.\n   103→  owner: EthereumAddress;\n   104→  traits: PassTrait[];\n   105→  /** Unix millis where the pass began staking. 0 means not currently staked. */\n   106→  stakedAt: number;\n   107→  /** Total governance voting power accumulated by this pass. */\n   108→  governancePower: number;\n   109→  /** URI pointing to the IPFS JSON metadata. */\n   110→  metadataUri: string;\n   111→  /** Logical deletion flag. ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   127→  /*                                  Factory                               */\n   128→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   129→\n   130→  static mint(props: {\n   131→    showId: string;\n   132→    tokenId: bigint;\n   133→    initialOwner: Date(now),\n   157→    });\n   158→\n   159→    return entity;\n   160→  }\n   161→\n   162→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   163→  /*                               Constructor                              */\n   164→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   165→\n   166→  /** Globally unique reference ID used internally/off-chain. */\n   167→  readonly id: string;\n   168→\n   169→  private constructor(private readonly props: ShowPassProps) {\n   170→    this.id = uuid(); // Off-chain identifier (NOT the           */\n   175→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   176→\n   177→  get tokenId(): bigint {\n   178→    return this.props.tokenId;\n   179→  }\n The previous value is overwritten and a new\n   216→   * `lastUpdated` timestamp is recorded.\n   217→   */\n   218→  evolve(traitUpdates: Record<string, string | number | boolean>): void {\n   219→    if (this.props.burned) throw new DomainError('Cannot evolve a burned pass');\n   220→\n   221→    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();\n   222→\n   223→    Object.entries(traitUpdates).forEach(([key, value]) => {\n   224→      const existing = this.props.traits.find((t) => t.key === key);\n   225→      if (existing) {\n   226→        existing.value = value;\n   227→        existing.lastUpdated = nowIso;\n   228→      } else {\n   229→        this.props.traits.push({ key, value, lastUpdated: nowIso });\n   230→      }\n   231→    });\n   232→\n   233→    this.touch();\n   234→\n   235→    DomainEvents.publish({\n   236→      name: 'ShowPassEvolved',\n   237→      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, traitUpdates },\n   238→      occurredOn: new Date(),\n   239→    });\n   240→  }\n   241→\n   242→  /**\n   243→   * Stake the pass. Throws if already staked or burned.\n   244→   */\n   245→  stake(): void {\n   246→    if (this.props.burned) throw new DomainError('Cannot stake a burned pass');\n   247→    if (this.isStaked) throw new DomainError('Pass already staked');\n   248→\n   249→    this.props.stakedAt = Date.now();\n   250→    this.touch();\n   251→\n   252→    DomainEvents.publish({\n   253→      name: 'ShowPassStaked',\n   254→      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, stakedAt: this.props.stakedAt },\n   255→      occurredOn: new Date(this.props.stakedAt),\n   256→    });\n   257→  }\n   258→\n   259→  /**\n   260→   * Un-stake the pass, awarding governance power proportional to duration.\n   261→   */\n   262→  unstake(): void {\n   263→    if (!this.isStaked) throw new DomainError('Pass is not currently staked');\n   264→\n   265→    const now = Date.now();\n   266→    const durationMs = now - this.props.stakedAt;\n   267→    const earnedPower = this.computeGovernancePower(durationMs);\n   268→    this.props.governancePower += earnedPower;\n   269→\n   270→    this.props.stakedAt = 0;\n   271→    this.touch();\n   272→\n   273→    DomainEvents.publish({\n   274→      name: 'ShowPassUnstaked',\n   275→      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, earnedPower },\n   276→      occurredOn: new Date(now),\n   277→    });\n   278→  }\n   279→\n   280→  /**\n   281→   * Transfer pass ownership. Does NOT perform on-chain transfer—that is handled\n   282→   * elsewhere. Unstake first.');\n   292→    }\n   293→\n   294→    const prevOwner = this.props.owner;\n   295→    this.props.owner = newOwner;\n   296→    this.touch();\n   297→\n   298→    DomainEvents.publish({\n   299→      name: 'ShowPassTransferred',\n   300→      payload: {\n   301→        tokenId: this.props.tokenId,\n   302→        from: prevOwner,\n   303→        to: newOwner,\n   304→      },\n   305→      occurredOn: new Date(),\n   306→    });\n   307→  }\n   308→\n   309→  /**\n   310→   * Burn (invalidate) the pass, preventing further interactions.  Once burned,\n   311→   * the token cannot be resurrected.\n   312→   */\n   313→  burn(reason?: string): void {\n   314→    if (this.props.burned) return;\n   315→\n   316→    if (this.isStaked) {\n   317→      throw new DomainError('Cannot burn a staked pass. Unstake first.');\n   318→    }\n   319→\n   320→    this.props.burned = true;\n   321→    this.touch();\n   322→\n   323→    DomainEvents.publish({\n   324→      name: 'ShowPassBurned',\n   325→      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, reason },\n   326→      occurredOn: new Date(),\n   327→    });\n   328→  }\n   329→\n   330→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   331→  /*                               Internals                                */\n   332→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   333→\n   334→  private computeGovernancePower(durationMs: number): number {\n   335→    // Governance power is 1 point per full hour staked, capped at 1,000\n   336→    const hours = Math.floor(durationMs / 3_600_000);\n   337→    return Math.min(hours, 1_000);\n   338→  }\n   339→\n   340→  private touch(): void {\n   341→    this.props.updatedAt = Date.now();\n   342→  }\n   343→\n   344→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   345→  /*                               Serialization                             */\n   346→  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   347→\n   348→  /**\n   349→   * Convert the entity to a plain JSON-serializable object for persistence or\n   350→   * wire transport. It lives purely\n * in the domain layer and knows nothing about how/where it will be persisted\n * or minted on-chain.  All side-effects are exposed via Domain Events so that\n * application services and infrastructure adapters can react appropriately.\n */\n\nimport { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                              Utility / Helpers                             */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntype EthereumAddress = `0x${string}`;\n\n/**\n * Very small address validator.  In a real code-base we might depend on\n * `ethers.utils.isAddress`, but the domain layer avoids heavy libs.\n */\nfunction assertValidAddress(address: string): asserts address is EthereumAddress {\n  const re = /^0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}$/;\n  if (!re.test(address)) {\n    throw new DomainError(`Invalid Ethereum address: ${address}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                  Errors                                    */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport class DomainError extends Error {\n  readonly name = 'DomainError';\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Domain Events                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport interface DomainEvent<T = unknown> {\n  readonly name: string;\n  readonly payload: T;\n  readonly occurredOn: Date;\n}\n\n/**\n * A super-lightweight synchronous event dispatcher used only in the domain\n * layer to register side-effect-free callbacks. Infrastructure code can wire\n * this up to a real bus (RabbitMQ, SNS, etc.) at the application boundary.\n */\nexport class DomainEvents {\n  private static handlers: Map<string, Set<(event: DomainEvent) => void>> =\n    new Map();\n\n  static publish(event: DomainEvent): void {\n    const listeners = this.handlers.get(event.name);\n    if (!listeners?.size) return;\n\n    listeners.forEach((cb) => {\n      try {\n        cb(event);\n      } catch (err) {\n        /* eslint-disable no-console */\n        console.error(`[DomainEvents] Handler threw for event ${event.name}`, err);\n        /* eslint-enable  no-console */\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  static subscribe(eventName: string, cb: (event: DomainEvent) => void): void {\n    const listeners = this.handlers.get(eventName) ?? new Set();\n    listeners.add(cb);\n    this.handlers.set(eventName, listeners);\n  }\n\n  static clear(): void {\n    this.handlers.clear();\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Value Objects                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/** A trait is a mutable property embedded in token metadata. */\nexport interface PassTrait {\n  key: string;\n  value: string | number | boolean;\n  /** RFC-3339 ISO string; easier to serialize than Date. */\n  lastUpdated: string;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                Aggregate                                   */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ninterface ShowPassProps {\n  tokenId: bigint; // On-chain tokenId (ERC-721 compatible).\n  showId: string; // UUID of the Show aggregate this pass belongs to.\n  owner: EthereumAddress;\n  traits: PassTrait[];\n  /** Unix millis where the pass began staking. 0 means not currently staked. */\n  stakedAt: number;\n  /** Total governance voting power accumulated by this pass. */\n  governancePower: number;\n  /** URI pointing to the IPFS JSON metadata. */\n  metadataUri: string;\n  /** Logical deletion flag. Burned tokens keep historical presence off-chain. */\n  burned: boolean;\n  /** Creation timestamp captured for audit trails. */\n  createdAt: number;\n  /** Last domain change timestamp. */\n  updatedAt: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Aggregate root representing a Show Pass.\n *\n * All state-mutating methods are synchronous and perform validation up-front,\n * emitting immutable Domain Events afterwards.\n */\nexport class ShowPass {\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                                  Factory                               */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  static mint(props: {\n    showId: string;\n    tokenId: bigint;\n    initialOwner: EthereumAddress;\n    metadataUri: string;\n  }): ShowPass {\n    assertValidAddress(props.initialOwner);\n\n    const now = Date.now();\n\n    const entity = new ShowPass({\n      tokenId: props.tokenId,\n      showId: props.showId,\n      owner: props.initialOwner,\n      traits: [],\n      stakedAt: 0,\n      governancePower: 0,\n      metadataUri: props.metadataUri,\n      burned: false,\n      createdAt: now,\n      updatedAt: now,\n    });\n\n    DomainEvents.publish({\n      name: 'ShowPassMinted',\n      payload: { showId: props.showId, tokenId: props.tokenId, owner: props.initialOwner },\n      occurredOn: new Date(now),\n    });\n\n    return entity;\n  }\n\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                               Constructor                              */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  /** Globally unique reference ID used internally/off-chain. */\n  readonly id: string;\n\n  private constructor(private readonly props: ShowPassProps) {\n    this.id = uuid(); // Off-chain identifier (NOT the on-chain tokenId).\n  }\n\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                            Read-only getters                           */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  get tokenId(): bigint {\n    return this.props.tokenId;\n  }\n\n  get showId(): string {\n    return this.props.showId;\n  }\n\n  get owner(): EthereumAddress {\n    return this.props.owner;\n  }\n\n  get traits(): readonly PassTrait[] {\n    // Expose an immutable snapshot\n    return [...this.props.traits];\n  }\n\n  get governancePower(): number {\n    return this.props.governancePower;\n  }\n\n  get isStaked(): boolean {\n    return this.props.stakedAt > 0;\n  }\n\n  get isBurned(): boolean {\n    return this.props.burned;\n  }\n\n  get metadataUri(): string {\n    return this.props.metadataUri;\n  }\n\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                              Mutations                                 */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  /**\n   * Update or append trait(s). The previous value is overwritten and a new\n   * `lastUpdated` timestamp is recorded.\n   */\n  evolve(traitUpdates: Record<string, string | number | boolean>): void {\n    if (this.props.burned) throw new DomainError('Cannot evolve a burned pass');\n\n    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();\n\n    Object.entries(traitUpdates).forEach(([key, value]) => {\n      const existing = this.props.traits.find((t) => t.key === key);\n      if (existing) {\n        existing.value = value;\n        existing.lastUpdated = nowIso;\n      } else {\n        this.props.traits.push({ key, value, lastUpdated: nowIso });\n      }\n    });\n\n    this.touch();\n\n    DomainEvents.publish({\n      name: 'ShowPassEvolved',\n      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, traitUpdates },\n      occurredOn: new Date(),\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Stake the pass. Throws if already staked or burned.\n   */\n  stake(): void {\n    if (this.props.burned) throw new DomainError('Cannot stake a burned pass');\n    if (this.isStaked) throw new DomainError('Pass already staked');\n\n    this.props.stakedAt = Date.now();\n    this.touch();\n\n    DomainEvents.publish({\n      name: 'ShowPassStaked',\n      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, stakedAt: this.props.stakedAt },\n      occurredOn: new Date(this.props.stakedAt),\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Un-stake the pass, awarding governance power proportional to duration.\n   */\n  unstake(): void {\n    if (!this.isStaked) throw new DomainError('Pass is not currently staked');\n\n    const now = Date.now();\n    const durationMs = now - this.props.stakedAt;\n    const earnedPower = this.computeGovernancePower(durationMs);\n    this.props.governancePower += earnedPower;\n\n    this.props.stakedAt = 0;\n    this.touch();\n\n    DomainEvents.publish({\n      name: 'ShowPassUnstaked',\n      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, earnedPower },\n      occurredOn: new Date(now),\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Transfer pass ownership. Does NOT perform on-chain transfer—that is handled\n   * elsewhere.  Domain validation ensures address format and non-burned state.\n   */\n  transfer(newOwner: EthereumAddress): void {\n    assertValidAddress(newOwner);\n\n    if (this.props.burned) throw new DomainError('Cannot transfer a burned pass');\n    if (this.owner.toLowerCase() === newOwner.toLowerCase()) return; // no-op\n\n    if (this.isStaked) {\n      throw new DomainError('Cannot transfer a staked pass. Unstake first.');\n    }\n\n    const prevOwner = this.props.owner;\n    this.props.owner = newOwner;\n    this.touch();\n\n    DomainEvents.publish({\n      name: 'ShowPassTransferred',\n      payload: {\n        tokenId: this.props.tokenId,\n        from: prevOwner,\n        to: newOwner,\n      },\n      occurredOn: new Date(),\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Burn (invalidate) the pass, preventing further interactions.  Once burned,\n   * the token cannot be resurrected.\n   */\n  burn(reason?: string): void {\n    if (this.props.burned) return;\n\n    if (this.isStaked) {\n      throw new DomainError('Cannot burn a staked pass. Unstake first.');\n    }\n\n    this.props.burned = true;\n    this.touch();\n\n    DomainEvents.publish({\n      name: 'ShowPassBurned',\n      payload: { tokenId: this.props.tokenId, reason },\n      occurredOn: new Date(),\n    });\n  }\n\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                               Internals                                */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  private computeGovernancePower(durationMs: number): number {\n    // Governance power is 1 point per full hour staked, capped at 1,000\n    const hours = Math.floor(durationMs / 3_600_000);\n    return Math.min(hours, 1_000);\n  }\n\n  private touch(): void {\n    this.props.updatedAt = Date.now();\n  }\n\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                               Serialization                             */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  /**\n   * Convert the entity to a plain JSON-serializable object for persistence or\n   * wire transport.  No functions or private state are leaked.\n   */\n  toJSON(): Readonly<ShowPassProps & { id: string }> {\n    return Object.freeze({\n      id: this.id,\n      ...this.props,\n      // Ensure we deep-clone nested arrays/objects for true immutability.\n      traits: this.props.traits.map((t) => ({ ...t })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Re-hydrate an entity from a persisted snapshot.  Only the Repository inside\n   * the infrastructure layer should call this.\n   */\n  static fromPersistence(raw: ShowPassProps & { id: string }): ShowPass {\n    const entity = new ShowPass({ ...raw });\n    (entity as { id: string }).id = raw.id; // restore deterministic id\n    return entity;\n  }\n}\n\n```","numLines":372,"startLine":1,"totalLines":372}}}

this.passRepo.update(pass);\n    } catch (err) {\n      return left(\n        new DomainError(\n          'STORAGE_ERROR',\n          'IPFS pinning failed: ' + (err as Error).message\n        )\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------ Smart-Contract / Blockchain ---------------------- */\n    try {\n      const txHash = await this.blockchain.mintNFT({\n        to: pass.ownerWallet,\n        tokenURI: pass.tokenURI,\n        tier: pass.tier,\n        txOptions: command.txOptions\n      });\n\n      pass.setMintTxHash(txHash);\n      await this.passRepo.update(pass);\n    } catch (err) {\n      // Rollback DB on chain failure\n      await this.passRepo.delete(pass.id).catch(() => void 0);\n      return left(\n        new DomainError(\n          'BLOCKCHAIN_ERROR',\n          'Mint transaction failed: ' + (err as Error).message\n        )\n      );\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------- Domain Events --------------------------- */\n    await this.eventBus.publish('pass.minted', {\n      passId: pass.id,\n      showId: pass.showId,\n      owner: pass.ownerWallet,\n      tier: pass.tier\n    });\n\n    return right(pass);\n  }\n\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n  /*                               Utilities                                */\n  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n  private validateCommand(command: MintShowPassCommand): DomainError | null {\n    if (!command.showId) {\n      return new DomainError('VALIDATION_ERROR', 'showId is required');\n    }\n    if (!command.ownerWallet) {\n      return new DomainError('VALIDATION_ERROR', 'ownerWallet is required');\n    }\n    if (!PassTier.isValid(command.tier)) {\n      return new DomainError(\n        'VALIDATION_ERROR',\n        `tier \"${command.tier}\" is invalid`\n      );\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n```\n","numLines":171,"startLine":1,"totalLines":171}}}

Verified: ${address}`));\n  } catch (err: any) {\n    // Already verified or other non-critical error\n    if (err.message?.toLowerCase().includes('already verified')) {\n (!existsSync(DEPLOYMENTS_DIR)) {\n    mkdirSync(DEPLOYMENTS_DIR);\n  }\n\n  records[contractName] = details;\n  writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify(records, null, 2));\n  console.log(chalk.green(`📒  Saved deployment: ${filePath}`));\n}\n\n/**\n * Attempt to verify contract source on Etherscan / Blockscout.\n */\nasync function verifyContract(address: string, constructorArgs: any[]): Promise<void> {\n  if (!process.env.ETHERSCAN_API_KEY) {\n    console.log(chalk.yellow('⚠️  Skipping verification – ETHERSCAN_API_KEY not set'));\n    return;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    await run('verify:verify', { address, constructorArguments: constructorArgs });\n    console.log(chalk.green(`🔍  Verified: ${address}`));\n  } catch (err: any) {\n    // Already verified or other non-critical error\n    if (err.message?.toLowerCase().includes('already verified')) {\n      console.log(chalk.cyan('ℹ️   Already verified'));\n    } else {\n      console.error(chalk.red(`Verification failed: ${err.message}`));\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Deploy an upgradeable proxy + implementation pair using the OpenZeppelin plugin.\n */\nasync function deployProxy(\n  contractName: string,\n  constructorArgs: any[] = [],\n  initializer = 'initialize',\n) {\n  console.log(chalk.blue(`🚀  Deploying ${contractName} Proxy…`));\n\n  const Factory = await ethers.getContractFactory(contractName);\n  const instance = await upgrades.deployProxy(Factory, constructorArgs, {\n    initializer,\n    kind: 'uups',\n  });\n\n  await instance.deployed();\n  const proxyAddress = instance.address;\n\n  // Fetch implementation address for records\n  const implAddress = await upgrades.erc1967.getImplementationAddress(proxyAddress);\n  const receipt = await instance.deployTransaction.wait();\n\n  console.log(\n    chalk.green(`✅  ${contractName} deployed`),\n    '\\n   ↳ proxy          :', proxyAddress,\n    '\\n   ↳ implementation :', implAddress,\n    '\\n   ↳ tx hash        :', receipt.transactionHash,\n  );\n\n  // Persist to filesystem\n  saveDeployment(receipt.network.chainId, contractName, {\n    proxy: proxyAddress,\n    implementation: implAddress,\n    txHash: receipt.transactionHash,\n    block: receipt.blockNumber,\n  });\n\n  // Verify implementation (proxy has no source)\n  await verifyContract(implAddress, []);\n\n  return instance;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Main ---------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  /* 1. CLI & network sanity checks ------------------------------------------------*/\n  const argv = yargs(hideBin(process.argv))\n    .option('verify', {\n      type: 'boolean',\n      default: true,\n      description: 'Verify contracts after deployment',\n    })\n    .parseSync();\n\n  const [deployer] = await ethers.getSigners();\n\n  console.log(chalk.magenta('🏗  StellarStage Carnival — Contract Deployment'));\n  console.log(`\\nNetwork     : ${network.name} (${await deployer.getChainId()})`);\n  console.log(`Deployer    : ${deployer.address}`);\n  console.log(`Balance     : ${ethers.utils.formatEther(await deployer.getBalance())} ETH\\n`);\n\n  /* 2. Deploy contracts ----------------------------------------------------------*/\n  // a) ShowPass (ERC-721 upgradable)\n  const showPass = await deployProxy('ShowPass', [\n    'StellarStage Show Pass',  // name\n    'SSC',                     // symbol\n    'ipfs://base-uri',         // base tokenURI\n  ]);\n\n  // b) LootVault (ERC-1155 loot drops)\n  const lootVault = await deployProxy('LootVault', []);\n\n  // c) CarnivalGovernor (Governor & Timelock compound)\n  const timelockDelay = 60 * 60 * 24; // 24h\n  const governor = await deployProxy('CarnivalGovernor', [\n    showPass.address,\n    timelockDelay,\n  ]);\n\n  // d) Registry (ties everything together)\n  const registry = await deployProxy('ShowRegistry', [\n    showPass.address,\n    lootVault.address,\n    governor.address,\n  ]);\n\n  /* 3. Post-deployment hooks -----------------------------------------------------*/\n  console.log(chalk.cyan('\\n🔗  Wiring contract references…'));\n  await (await showPass.setRegistry(registry.address)).wait();\n  await (await lootVault.setRegistry(registry.address)).wait();\n\n  console.log(chalk.green('🎉  Deployment complete!'));\n}\n\n/* ---------- Bootstrap -----------------------------------------------------------*/\n\nmain().catch((err) => {\n  console.error(chalk.red('Fatal deployment error:\\n'), err);\n  process.exitCode = 1;\n});\n```\n```typescript\n/**\n * StellarStage Carnival – Database Seeder\n *\n * This script bootstraps a brand-new database with canonical data that the\n * platform relies on to operate in non-production environments (local, CI,\n * staging).
