Prerequisites  \n2. Clone & Bootstrap  \n3. Environment Variables  \n4. Workspace Layout  \n5. Common `package.json` Scripts  \n6. Run the Full Stack (DEV)  \n7. Deploy Contracts to Testnet  \n8. Generate Types from ABI  \n9. Database & Subgraph  \n10. Linting, Tests & Coverage  \n11. CI / CD Pipelines  \n12. Troubleshooting  \n\n---\n\n## 1. Prerequisites\n| Tool              | Version (min) | Notes                                    |\n| ----------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------- |\n| Node.js           | `18.x LTS`    | Works on 20.x too, but 18 is enforced    |\n| pnpm              | `^8.0.0`      | We use pnpm workspaces                   |\n| Docker & Compose  | `>= 24`       | Needed for Postgres, IPFS, Graph Node    |\n| Foundry           | `>= 0.2.0`    | Ultra-fast Rust-based Solidity toolchain |\n| Git               | `>= 2.30`     |                                         |\n\n```bash\n# macOS (using asdf)\nbrew install asdf\nasdf plugin-add nodejs\nasdf install nodejs latest:18\nnpm i -g pnpm@latest\n\n# Foundry (Linux / macOS)\ncurl -L https://foundry.paradigm.xyz | bash && foundryup\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Clone & Bootstrap\n\n```bash\ngit clone git@github.com:StellarStage/stage-carnival.git\ncd stage-carnival\n\n# install all workspace deps in parallel\npnpm install --frozen-lockfile\n\n# bootstrap TypeScript project references\npnpm run build:types\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Environment Variables\n\nCopy the example file and edit values as needed:\n\n```bash\ncp .env.example .env\n```\n\n```dotenv\n############################################################\n# Blockchain                                              ##\n############################################################\nRPC_URL=https://rpc.goerli.eth.gateway.fm\nPRIVATE_KEY=0xYOUR_PRIVATE_KEY\nCHAIN_ID=5\nIPFS_API=https://ipfs.infura.io:5001\n\n############################################################\n# Database / Cache                                        ##\n############################################################\nPOSTGRES_URL=postgres://carnival:carnival@localhost:5432/carnival\nREDIS_URL=redis://localhost:6379\n\n############################################################\n# Front-End                                               ##\n############################################################\nVITE_PUBLIC_GRAPHQL_URL=http://localhost:4000/graphql\nVITE_PUBLIC_SOCKET_URL=ws://localhost:4001\n```\n\nTip: Never commit `.env` – it’s ignored by `.gitignore` and checked in CI via [dotenv-safe](https://www.npmjs.com/package/dotenv-safe).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Workspace Layout\n\n```\n.\n├── apps/\n│   ├── frontend/         # React + Three.js client\n│   ├── api/              # Fastify + GraphQL server\n│   └── subgraph/         # The Graph manifest & mappings\n├── packages/\n│   ├── contracts/        # Solidity sources & tests\n│   ├── core/             # Domain entities & use-cases\n│   ├── adapters/         # Ethereum, IPFS, Stream infra\n│   └── shared/           # UI libs, config utils, ts-configs\n└── infra/\n    ├── docker/           # Dockerfiles / compose\n    └── k8s/              # Helm charts\n```\n\nAll packages rely on strict [TypeScript Project References](https://www.typescriptlang.org/docs/handbook/project-references.html) to enforce proper boundaries between **Domain**, **Use-Cases**, and **Adapters**.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Common `package.json` Scripts\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"dev\":        \"pnpm -r --parallel run dev\",\n    \"test\":       \"pnpm -r --parallel run test\",\n    \"lint\":       \"eslint \\\"{apps,packages}/**/*.{ts,tsx}\\\" --fix\",\n    \"format\":     \"prettier -w \\\"**/*.{ts,tsx,md,json,yml}\\\"\",\n    \"typecheck\":  \"tsc -b\",\n    \"coverage\":   \"vitest run --coverage\",\n    \"deploy\":     \"pnpm --filter contracts exec tsx scripts/deploy.ts\",\n    \"graph:codegen\": \"graph codegen && graph build\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\nRun any script at the workspace root or target a specific package via `--filter`.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Run the Full Stack (DEV)\n\n1. Spin up infra services:\n\n```bash\ndocker compose -f infra/docker/docker-compose.dev.yml up -d\n```\n\nThis brings up:\n* Postgres + PGAdmin  \n* Redis  \n* IPFS  \n* Local Graph Node  \n* Hardhat node (if not running Foundry’s `anvil`)  \n\n2. In a new terminal, start everything else:\n\n```bash\npnpm dev\n```\n\n`pnpm` will:\n* compile and deploy the smart contracts to your local chain  \n* launch the API server on `localhost:4000`  \n* start the WebSocket bus on `4001`  \n* spin up the React/Three front-end on `5173` (Vite)  \n\nHot-reloading is wired end-to-end – as soon as you tweak an NFT trait strategy or GraphQL resolver, the UI updates in place.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Deploy Contracts to Testnet\n\nWe use Foundry for compilation & Hardhat scripts for deployment:\n\n```bash\n# compile with foundry (fast)\npnpm --filter contracts run build\n\n# deploy with Ethers.js + Hardhat to Goerli\nCHAIN_ID=5 \\\nRPC_URL=https://rpc.goerli.eth.gateway.fm \\\nPRIVATE_KEY=0xyourkey \\\npnpm --filter contracts exec tsx scripts/deploy.ts\n```\n\nThe script outputs a fully typed `broadcast/5/latest-deployments.json` map, automatically consumed by the front-end via the `@carnival/contracts` package.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Generate Types from ABI\n\nAny time the Solidity sources change, run:\n\n```bash\npnpm --filter contracts run build        # ensure fresh ABIs\npnpm --filter contracts exec tsx scripts/typegen.ts\n```\n\n`typegen.ts` leverages `@typechain/ethers-v6` to emit type-safe contract wrappers used across the entire monorepo.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7e9926cd-6518-4883-956a-03ca733fb24c","uuid":"e264d072-2f77-4579-9f9f-dd80fbd2036c","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"total 36\ndrwxrwxr-x 2 root root 4096 Feb  2 19:39 .\ndrwxr-xr-x 6 root root 4096 Feb  2 19:39 ..\n-rw-rw-r-- 1 root root 8021 Feb  1 02:26 01_PROJECT_SETUP.md\n-rw-rw-r-- 1 root root 8755 Feb  1 02:26 02_DEPLOYMENT.md\n-rw-rw-r-- 1 root root 7085 Feb  1 02:26 03_CONTRIBUTING.md\n```markdown\n# StellarStage Carnival – Project Setup Guide\n> docs/guides/01_PROJECT_SETUP.md  \n> Version: 1.0.0 • Last updated: 2024-05-02\n\nWelcome to the StellarStage Carnival monorepo!  \nThis guide walks you through installing dependencies, configuring your local blockchain stack, and running all services (smart contracts, back-end, front-end, and real-time infra) in under **10 minutes**.\n\n---\n\n## Table of Contents\n1. -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nfunction mapDomainError(err: unknown): ApolloError {\n  if (err instanceof DomainError) {\n    // Domain errors are                                  */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport const passResolver: IResolvers<unknown, GraphQLContext> = {\n  /* hasNextPage: result.hasNextPage,\n            endCursor: result.endCursor,\n          },\n          totalCount: result.totalCount,\n        };\n      } catch (err) {\n        throw mapDomainError(err);\n      }\n    },\n  },\n\n  /* ============================ Root Mutations ============================ */\n\n  Mutation: {\n    /**\n     * mintPass(): Mints a new show pass  },\n      ctx,\n    ) {\n      if (!ctx.user) {\n        throw new AuthenticationError('Not authenticated');\n      }\n\n      try {\n        const useCase = container.resolve<MintShowPassUseCase>('MintShowPassUseCase');\n        const pass = await useCase.execute({\n          ownerId: ctx.user.id,\n          showId: args.input.showId,\n          seat: args.input.seat,\n          tier: args.input.tier,\n        });\n\n        const dto = PassDTOMapper.toDTO(pass);\n        void pubSub.publish(PASS_MINTED_EVENT, { passMinted: dto });\n        return Upgrade Mechanism Identification\n\n### 1.1 Pattern Analysis\n\nBased on analysis of the contract architecture, **StellarStage Carnival uses a UUPS (Universal Upgradeable Proxy Standard) pattern** for the `ShowPass.sol` contract, not a Transparent Proxy pattern as suggested in some documentation.\n\n**Evidence from `contracts/core/ShowPass.sol:26,45,287-291`:**\n\n```solidity\nimport {UUPSUpgradeable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/proxy/utils/UUPSUpgradeable.sol\";\n\ncontract ShowPass is\n    ERC721EnumerableUpgradeable,\n    ERC2981Upgradeable,\n    AccessControlUpgradeable,\n    PausableUpgradeable,\n    ReentrancyGuardUpgradeable,\n    UUPSUpgradeable\n{\n    // ...\n\n    /// UUPS upgradability\n    function _authorizeUpgrade(address newImplementation)\n        internal\n        override\n        onlyRole(ADMIN_ROLE)\n    {}\n}\n```\n\n### 1.2 Key Characteristics of the UUPS Pattern\n\n| Aspect | Description |\n|--------|-------------|\n| **Upgrade Control** | Logic resides in the implementation contract itself (`_authorizeUpgrade`) |\n| **Authorization** | Protected by `ADMIN_ROLE` access control |\n| **Storage Slot** | Uses EIP-1967 standard slot: `0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc` |\n| **Proxy Contract** | `contracts/proxies/ShowPassProxy.sol` delegates calls to implementation |\n| **Timelock** | 48-hour mandatory delay before upgrade execution (see `ShowPassProxy.sol:56`) |\n\n### 1.3 Proxy Architecture Details\n\nFrom `contracts/proxies/ShowPassProxy.sol:28-88`:\n\n- The proxy uses a **two-step upgrade process**: `proposeUpgrade()` followed by `upgradeTo()` after timelock expiration\n- Custom EIP-1967 compliant storage slots for admin, implementation, and upgrade proposal\n- 48-hour minimum timelock (`MIN_TIMELOCK = 48 hours`)\n- The proxy admin can call `upgradeToAndCall()` to atomically upgrade and initialize the new implementation\n\n---\n\n## 2. Off-Chain Dependencies Analysis\n\n### 2.1 Critical Backend Components Dependent on ShowPass ABI\n\nThe following files in `packages/backend` are tightly coupled to the `ShowPass.sol` contract's ABI and storage layout:\n\n#### 2.1.1 `packages/backend/src/infrastructure/adapters/blockchain/ethers.service.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Core blockchain interaction layer that instantiates contract objects using ABIs.\n\n**Key Dependencies (lines 53-57, 283-289):**\n- `loadContract<T>()` method takes an ABI parameter to create ethers.js Contract instances\n- Any new functions or events added to ShowPass will require ABI updates here\n- The service caches contract instances, requiring service restart after ABI changes\n\n**Why it matters:** This service directly calls contract functions like `mint()`, `grantXP()`, `stake()`, `unstake()`. If the contract's function signatures change (e.g., adding royalty tier parameters), this adapter must be updated with the new ABI.\n\n#### 2.1.2 `packages/backend/src/infrastructure/adapters/blockchain/contract.mapper.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Bridges domain entities with low-level EVM contract calls and translates on-chain events to domain events.\n\n**Key Dependencies (lines 52-68, 125-151, 184-243):**\n\n```typescript\nconst ABI_FRAGMENT: Record<'ShowPass' | 'Loot' | 'Governance', ContractInterface> = {\n  ShowPass: [\n    'function safeMint(address to, uint256 tokenId, string memory uri) public',\n    'event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 indexed tokenId)',\n  ],\n  // ...\n};\n```\n\n**Why it matters:**\n- Contains hardcoded ABI fragments for ShowPass operations\n- The `toDomainEvent()` method (lines 184-243) parses on-chain events - if new royalty-related events are added, this mapper must be extended\n- The `toMintShowPassTx()` method encodes function calls - any parameter changes require updates here\n\n#### 2.1.3 `packages/backend/src/application/observers/stage-event.observer.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Listens to domain events and broadcasts them to external delivery mechanisms (WebSockets, GraphQL subscriptions).\n\n**Key Dependencies (lines 74-75, 217-233):**\n\n```typescript\ncase PassStakedEvent:\n  await this.handlePassStaked(event as PassStakedEvent);\n  break;\n```\n\n**Why it matters:**\n- If the new tiered royalty system introduces new events (e.g., `RoyaltyTierUpdated`), this observer must be extended to handle them\n- Event payload structures must match what the contract emits\n\n#### 2.1.4 `packages/backend/src/domain/entities/show-pass.entity.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Domain-layer entity representing a ShowPass NFT.\n\n**Key Dependencies (lines 100-117):**\n\n```typescript\ninterface ShowPassProps {\n  tokenId: bigint;\n  showId: string;\n  owner: EthereumAddress;\n  traits: PassTrait[];\n  stakedAt: number;\n  governancePower: number;\n  metadataUri: string;\n  burned: boolean;\n  createdAt: number;\n  updatedAt: number;\n}\n```\n\n**Why it matters:** If the tiered royalty system introduces new on-chain state (e.g., `royaltyTier`, `royaltyPercentage`), the domain entity must be extended to reflect these properties for proper off-chain state synchronization.\n\n#### 2.1.5 `packages/backend/src/application/ports/IBlockchain.service.ts`\n\n**Purpose:** Clean Architecture port defining the blockchain interaction interface.\n\n**Key Dependencies (lines 39-46, 154-168):**\n\n```typescript\ninterface MintParams {\n  to: Address;\n  showId: string;\n  metadataUri: string;\n  traits: Record<string, any>;\n  nonce?: number;\n  value?: BigNumber;\n}\n```\n\n**Why it matters:** If minting now requires royalty tier selection, the `MintParams` interface must be extended, propagating changes to all implementing adapters.\n\n### 2.2 Configuration Dependencies\n\n| Component | File | Dependency Type |\n|-----------|------|-----------------|\n| Contract Addresses | `packages/backend/src/infrastructure/adapters/config/blockchain.config.ts` (referenced) | Proxy address storage |\n| TypeChain Types | `typechain/` directory | Auto-generated contract types |\n| ABI Files | `@/contracts/abi/ShowPassProxy.json` | Contract interface definition |\n| Environment | `.env` / `hardhat.config.ts` | Network & deployment settings |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upgrade Sequence\n\n### 3.1 Pre-Upgrade Phase (T-7 days to T-48h)\n\n| Step | Action | Owner | Verification |\n|------|--------|-------|--------------|\n| 1 | **Develop ShowPassV2.sol** with tiered royalty logic | Smart Contract Dev | Unit tests pass |\n| 2 | **Run storage layout compatibility check** | Smart Contract Dev | `npx hardhat storage:layout` |\n| 3 | **Verify storage gap adequacy** | Dev | Add royalty properties to `show-pass.entity.ts` |\n| 6 | **Update event observers** | Backend Dev | Handle new events in `stage-event.observer.ts` |\n| 7 | **Run full test suite** | QA | `pnpm test:contracts && pnpm test:backend` |\n| 8 | **Deploy to testnet (Mumbai/Goerli)** | DevOps | Smoke test with real transactions |\n\n### 3.2 Upgrade Proposal Phase (T-48h)\n\n| Step | Action | Command/Details |\n|------|--------|-----------------|\n| 1 | **Deploy new implementation** | `forge create ShowPassV2 --rpc-url $RPC_URL --private-key $PK` |\n| 2 | **Propose upgrade to proxy** | `ShowPassProxy.proposeUpgrade(newImplAddress)` |\n| 3 | **Verify implementation contract** | `npx hardhat verify --network mainnet <implAddress>` |\n| 4 | **Notify governance/DAO** | Create on-chain proposal if using `CarnivalGovernor` |\n| 5 | **Prepare backend deployment** | Build Docker images with updated ABIs |\n\n### 3.3 Upgrade Execution Phase (T-0)\n\n**CRITICAL: Execute in this exact order to prevent inconsistent state:**\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Admin\n    participant Proxy\n    participant Backend\n    participant EventBus\n\n    Note over Admin,EventBus: Phase 1: On-Chain Upgrade\n    Admin->>Proxy: Call upgradeTo(newImplementation)\n    Proxy->>Proxy: Verify timelock expired\n    Proxy->>Proxy: Update IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT\n    Proxy-->>Admin: Emit Upgraded event\n\n    Note over Admin,EventBus: Phase 2: Off-Chain Sync (within 5 min)\n    Admin->>Backend: Deploy updated backend services\n    Backend->>Backend: Load new ABI from config\n    Backend->>EventBus: Reconnect event listeners\n\n    Note over Admin,EventBus: Phase 3: Verification\n    Admin->>Proxy: Call version() to verify\n    Backend->>Proxy: Sync state via read calls\n```\n\n| Step | Action | Command/Details | Timeout |\n|------|--------|-----------------|---------|\n| 1 | **Execute on-chain upgrade** | `ShowPassProxy.upgradeTo(newImplAddress)` | TX confirmation |\n| 2 | **Pause backend services** | `kubectl scale deployment backend --replicas=0` | 30 seconds |\n| 3 | **Update ABI configuration** | Deploy new config map or env variable | 30 seconds |\n| 4 | **Redeploy backend services** | `kubectl apply -f backend-deployment.yaml` | 2 minutes |\n| 5 | **Verify contract version** | Call `version()` or read new state | Immediate |\n| 6 | **Run smoke tests** | `pnpm test:e2e --smoke` | 5 minutes |\n\n### 3.4 Post-Upgrade Phase\n\n| Step | Action | Verification |\n|------|--------|--------------|\n| 1 | **Monitor event ingestion** | Check `stage-event.observer.ts` logs |\n| 2 | **Verify royalty functionality** | Execute test transactions on mainnet |\n| 3 | **Update subgraph** (if applicable) | Redeploy Graph Node mappings |\n| 4 | **Update frontend** | Clear contract caches, update wagmi/viem config |\n| 5 | **Document changes** | Update `CHANGELOG.md` and architecture docs |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Risk Assessment and Mitigation\n\n### 4.1 Risk: Storage Layout Collision\n\n**Severity:** CRITICAL\n**Probability:** Medium (if not using storage gaps properly)\n\n**Description:** Adding new state variables to `ShowPassV2.sol` can corrupt existing storage if:\n- Variables are inserted before existing ones\n- The inheritance chain is modified\n- Storage slot calculations change\n\n**Evidence from `ShowPass.sol:66-70`:**\n```solidity\nCounters.Counter private _tokenIds;\nmapping(uint256 => PassData) internal _passData;\nstring private _baseTokenURI;\nuint32 private constant _XP_PER_LEVEL = 1_000 ether;\n```\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Always append new variables at the end** of the storage layout\n2. Use the existing `__gap` pattern (currently 49 slots reserved in documentation example)\n3. **Run storage verification before deployment:**\n   ```bash\n   npx hardhat storage:layout --contract ShowPass > v1-layout.json\n   npx hardhat storage:layout --contract ShowPassV2 > v2-layout.json\n   diff v1-layout.json v2-layout.json\n   ```\n4. Use OpenZeppelin's `@openzeppelin/upgrades-core` storage validation in CI:\n   ```javascript\n   const { validateUpgrade } = require('@openzeppelin/upgrades-core');\n   await validateUpgrade(ShowPassV1, ShowPassV2);\n   ```\n\n### 4.2 Risk: Backend ABI Desynchronization\n\n**Severity:** HIGH\n**Probability:** High (if backend not updated simultaneously)\n\n**Description:** If the backend services continue running with the old ABI after the on-chain upgrade:\n- Calls to new functions will fail\n- New events will be silently ignored\n- Data mapping will produce incorrect results\n\n**Affected Files:**\n- `contract.mapper.ts:52-68` (hardcoded ABI fragments)\n- `ethers.service.ts` (contract instantiation)\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Blue-Green Deployment:** Prepare the updated backend before the on-chain upgrade\n2. **Feature Flags:** Use environment variables to toggle between ABIs\n3. **Coordinated Cutover:**\n   ```bash\n   # Execute in rapid succession\n   TX_HASH=$(cast send $PROXY \"upgradeTo(address)\" $NEW_IMPL --private-key $PK)\n   cast receipt $TX_HASH --confirmations 2\n   kubectl rollout restart deployment/backend\n   ```\n4. **Health Checks:** Add a `/health/blockchain` endpoint that verifies ABI compatibility:\n   ```typescript\n   async checkABICompatibility(): Promise<boolean> {\n     const version = await this.contract.version();\n     return version === EXPECTED_CONTRACT_VERSION;\n   }\n   ```\n\n### 4.3 Risk: Event Listener Disruption\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Probability:** Medium\n\n**Description:** The `stage-event.observer.ts` subscribes to on-chain events. During the upgrade window:\n- Events emitted during the transition may be lost\n- New event types won't be recognized by old code\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Implement event replay mechanism:**\n   - Store the last processed block number\n   - On restart, replay events from that block\n2. **Add catch-all event handling:**\n   ```typescript\n   default:\n     this.logger.warn(`Unknown event type: ${event.name}`, { event });\n     // Don't throw - graceful degradation\n   ```\n3. **Use block-based synchronization** rather than real-time listeners for critical state\n\n### 4.4 Risk: Service Downtime\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Probability:** High (by design during rolling update)\n\n**Description:** Users may experience brief service interruption during the backend redeployment window.\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Schedule upgrade during low-traffic period**\n2. **Use Kubernetes rolling updates** with proper readiness probes\n3. **Maintain read-only mode** during upgrade:\n   - Frontend can still read contract state directly\n   - Queue write operations for replay after upgrade\n4. **Communicate maintenance window** to users in advance\n\n### 4.5 Risk: Governance Attack / Unauthorized Upgrade\n\n**Severity:** CRITICAL\n**Probability:** Low (if following security practices)\n\n**Description:** Malicious actor could propose a malicious implementation if they gain `ADMIN_ROLE`.\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Multi-sig requirement:** Use a Gnosis Safe with 5/9 threshold for admin operations\n2. **Timelock enforcement:** The 48-hour window allows community review\n3. **Implementation verification:** Always verify source code on Etherscan\n4. **Governance review:** Route significant upgrades through `CarnivalGovernor` DAO vote\n\n---\n\n## 5. Implementation Checklist\n\n### 5.1 Smart Contract Changes\n\n- [ ] Create `ShowPassV2.sol` extending `ShowPass.sol`\n- [ ] Add `royaltyTier` state variable (append to storage)\n- [ ] Implement `setRoyaltyTier()` with access control\n- [ ] Override `royaltyInfo()` to return tier-based percentages\n- [ ] Add `RoyaltyTierUpdated` event\n- [ ] Reduce `__gap` array size by variables added\n- [ ] Write comprehensive unit tests\n- [ ] Run storage layout validation\n\n### 5.2 Backend Changes\n\n- [ ] Update `ABI_FRAGMENT.ShowPass` in `contract.mapper.ts`\n- [ ] Add `royaltyTier` to `ShowPassProps` interface\n- [ ] Update `ShowPass` domain entity\n- [ ] Add `RoyaltyTierUpdatedEvent` handler in `stage-event.observer.ts`\n- [ ] Update `IBlockchainService` port if needed\n- [ ] Regenerate TypeChain types: `pnpm --filter contracts exec tsx scripts/typegen.ts`\n- [ ] Update integration tests\n\n### 5.3 DevOps Changes\n\n- [ ] Prepare Docker images with updated ABIs\n- [ ] Update Kubernetes manifests\n- [ ] Configure feature flags for gradual rollout\n- [ ] Prepare rollback scripts\n- [ ] Document runbook for on-call engineers\n\n---\n\n## 6. Rollback Procedure\n\nIn case of critical issues post-upgrade:\n\n### 6.1 Smart Contract Rollback\n\n```bash\n# Only if within acceptable risk window (no new state committed)\nnpx ts-node scripts/rollback.ts --proxy $PROXY_ADDR --prevImpl $V1_IMPL_ADDR\n```\n\n**Warning:** Rolling back after users have interacted with V2 functions may cause data inconsistency.\n\n### 6.2 Backend Rollback\n\n```bash\nkubectl rollout undo deployment/backend\n# Or explicitly:\nkubectl set image deployment/backend backend=carnival/backend:v1.0.0\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Conclusion\n\nThe addition of a tiered royalty system to `ShowPass.sol` is a moderate-complexity upgrade that requires careful coordination between on-chain and off-chain components. The UUPS proxy pattern provides flexibility, but demands strict adherence to storage layout rules.\n\n**Key Takeaways:**\n1. The upgrade mechanism is **UUPS** (not Transparent Proxy as some docs suggest)\n2. The backend has **three critical ABI-dependent files** that must be updated: `ethers.service.ts`, `contract.mapper.ts`, and `stage-event.observer.ts`\n3. The upgrade sequence must follow a **strict on-chain-first, off-chain-second** order\n4. **Storage collision** is the highest-risk issue and requires tooling-assisted validation\n5. A **48-hour timelock** provides a natural review window for governance\n\nThe recommended approach is to treat this as a coordinated release across all platform components, with comprehensive testing on Mumbai/Goerli testnet before mainnet deployment.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":121,"cache_read_input_tokens":95992,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":121,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7e9926cd-6518-4883-956a-03ca733fb24c","uuid":"362d30a0-c7c5-4532-817b-1612136b85ed"}

proposal if using `CarnivalGovernor` |\n| 5 | **Prepare backend deployment** | Build Docker images with updated ABIs |\n\n### 3.3 Upgrade Execution Phase (T-0)\n\n**CRITICAL: Execute in this exact order to prevent inconsistent state:**\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Admin\n    participant Proxy\n    participant Backend\n    participant EventBus\n\n    Note over Admin,EventBus: Phase 1: On-Chain Upgrade\n    Admin->>Proxy: Call upgradeTo(newImplementation)\n    Proxy->>Proxy: Verify timelock expired\n    Proxy->>Proxy: Update IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT\n    Proxy-->>Admin: Emit Upgraded event\n\n    Note over Admin,EventBus: Phase 2: Off-Chain Sync (within 5 min)\n    Admin->>Backend: Deploy updated backend services\n    Backend->>Backend: Load new ABI from config\n    Backend->>EventBus: Reconnect event listeners\n\n    Note over Admin,EventBus: Phase 3: Verification\n    Admin->>Proxy: Call version() to verify\n    Backend->>Proxy: Sync state via read calls\n```\n\n| Step | Action | Command/Details | Timeout |\n|------|--------|-----------------|---------|\n| 1 | **Execute on-chain upgrade** | `ShowPassProxy.upgradeTo(newImplAddress)` | TX confirmation |\n| 2 | **Pause backend services** | `kubectl scale deployment backend --replicas=0` | 30 seconds |\n| 3 | **Update ABI configuration** | Deploy new config map or env variable | 30 seconds |\n| 4 | **Redeploy backend services** | `kubectl apply -f backend-deployment.yaml` | 2 minutes |\n| 5 | **Verify contract version** | Call `version()` or read new state | Immediate |\n| 6 | **Run smoke tests** | `pnpm test:e2e --smoke` | 5 minutes |\n\n### 3.4 Post-Upgrade Phase\n\n| Step | Action | Verification |\n|------|--------|--------------|\n| 1 | **Monitor event ingestion** | Check `stage-event.observer.ts` logs |\n| 2 | **Verify royalty functionality** | Execute test transactions on mainnet |\n| 3 | **Update subgraph** (if applicable) | Redeploy Graph Node mappings |\n| 4 | **Update frontend** | Clear contract caches, update wagmi/viem config |\n| 5 | **Document changes** | Update `CHANGELOG.md` and architecture docs |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Risk Assessment and Mitigation\n\n### 4.1 Risk: Storage Layout Collision\n\n**Severity:** CRITICAL\n**Probability:** Medium (if not using storage gaps properly)\n\n**Description:** Adding new state variables to `ShowPassV2.sol` can corrupt existing storage if:\n- Variables are inserted before existing ones\n- The inheritance chain is modified\n- Storage slot calculations change\n\n**Evidence from `ShowPass.sol:66-70`:**\n```solidity\nCounters.Counter private _tokenIds;\nmapping(uint256 => PassData) internal _passData;\nstring private _baseTokenURI;\nuint32 private constant _XP_PER_LEVEL = 1_000 ether;\n```\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Always append new variables at the end** of the storage layout\n2. Use the existing `__gap` pattern (currently 49 slots reserved in documentation example)\n3. **Run storage verification before deployment:**\n   ```bash\n   npx hardhat storage:layout --contract ShowPass > v1-layout.json\n   npx hardhat storage:layout --contract ShowPassV2 > v2-layout.json\n   diff v1-layout.json v2-layout.json\n   ```\n4. Use OpenZeppelin's `@openzeppelin/upgrades-core` storage validation in CI:\n   ```javascript\n   const { validateUpgrade } = require('@openzeppelin/upgrades-core');\n   await validateUpgrade(ShowPassV1, ShowPassV2);\n   ```\n\n### 4.2 Risk: Backend ABI Desynchronization\n\n**Severity:** HIGH\n**Probability:** High (if backend not updated simultaneously)\n\n**Description:** If the backend services continue running with the old ABI after the on-chain upgrade:\n- Calls to new functions will fail\n- New events will be silently ignored\n- Data mapping will produce incorrect results\n\n**Affected Files:**\n- `contract.mapper.ts:52-68` (hardcoded ABI fragments)\n- `ethers.service.ts` (contract instantiation)\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Blue-Green Deployment:** Prepare the updated backend before the on-chain upgrade\n2. **Feature Flags:** Use environment variables to toggle between ABIs\n3. **Coordinated Cutover:**\n   ```bash\n   # Execute in rapid succession\n   TX_HASH=$(cast send $PROXY \"upgradeTo(address)\" $NEW_IMPL --private-key $PK)\n   cast receipt $TX_HASH --confirmations 2\n   kubectl rollout restart deployment/backend\n   ```\n4. **Health Checks:** Add a `/health/blockchain` endpoint that verifies ABI compatibility:\n   ```typescript\n   async checkABICompatibility(): Promise<boolean> {\n     const version = await this.contract.version();\n     return version === EXPECTED_CONTRACT_VERSION;\n   }\n   ```\n\n### 4.3 Risk: Event Listener Disruption\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Probability:** Medium\n\n**Description:** The `stage-event.observer.ts` subscribes to on-chain events. During the upgrade window:\n- Events emitted during the transition may be lost\n- New event types won't be recognized by old code\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Implement event replay mechanism:**\n   - Store the last processed block number\n   - On restart, replay events from that block\n2. **Add catch-all event handling:**\n   ```typescript\n   default:\n     this.logger.warn(`Unknown event type: ${event.name}`, { event });\n     // Don't throw - graceful degradation\n   ```\n3. **Use block-based synchronization** rather than real-time listeners for critical state\n\n### 4.4 Risk: Service Downtime\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Probability:** High (by design during rolling update)\n\n**Description:** Users may experience brief service interruption during the backend redeployment window.\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Schedule upgrade during low-traffic period**\n2. **Use Kubernetes rolling updates** with proper readiness probes\n3. **Maintain read-only mode** during upgrade:\n   - Frontend can still read contract state directly\n   - Queue write operations for replay after upgrade\n4. **Communicate maintenance window** to users in advance\n\n### 4.5 Risk: Governance Attack / Unauthorized Upgrade\n\n**Severity:** CRITICAL\n**Probability:** Low (if following security practices)\n\n**Description:** Malicious actor could propose a malicious implementation if they gain `ADMIN_ROLE`.\n\n**Mitigation Strategy:**\n1. **Multi-sig requirement:** Use a Gnosis Safe with 5/9 threshold for admin operations\n2. **Timelock enforcement:** The 48-hour window allows community review\n3. **Implementation verification:** Always verify source code on Etherscan\n4. **Governance review:** Route significant upgrades through `CarnivalGovernor` DAO vote\n\n---\n\n## 5. Implementation Checklist\n\n### 5.1 Smart Contract Changes\n\n- [ ] Create `ShowPassV2.sol` extending `ShowPass.sol`\n- [ ] Add `royaltyTier` state variable (append to storage)\n- [ ] Implement `setRoyaltyTier()` with access control\n- [ ] Override `royaltyInfo()` to return tier-based percentages\n- [ ] Add `RoyaltyTierUpdated` event\n- [ ] Reduce `__gap` array size by variables added\n- [ ] Write comprehensive unit tests\n- [ ] Run storage layout validation\n\n### 5.2 Backend Changes\n\n- [ ] Update `ABI_FRAGMENT.ShowPass` in `contract.mapper.ts`\n- [ ] Add `royaltyTier` to `ShowPassProps` interface\n- [ ] Update `ShowPass` domain entity\n- [ ] Add `RoyaltyTierUpdatedEvent` handler in `stage-event.observer.ts`\n- [ ] Update `IBlockchainService` port if needed\n- [ ] Regenerate TypeChain types: `pnpm --filter contracts exec tsx scripts/typegen.ts`\n- [ ] Update integration tests\n\n### 5.3 DevOps Changes\n\n- [ ] Prepare Docker images with updated ABIs\n- [ ] Update Kubernetes manifests\n- [ ] Configure feature flags for gradual rollout\n- [ ] Prepare rollback scripts\n- [ ] Document runbook for on-call engineers\n\n---\n\n## 6. Rollback Procedure\n\nIn case of critical issues post-upgrade:\n\n### 6.1 Smart Contract Rollback\n\n```bash\n# Only if within acceptable risk window (no new state committed)\nnpx ts-node scripts/rollback.ts --proxy $PROXY_ADDR --prevImpl $V1_IMPL_ADDR\n```\n\n**Warning:** Rolling back after users have interacted with V2 functions may cause data inconsistency.\n\n### 6.2 Backend Rollback\n\n```bash\nkubectl rollout undo deployment/backend\n# Or explicitly:\nkubectl set image deployment/backend backend=carnival/backend:v1.0.0\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. References\n\n- `contracts/core/ShowPass.sol` - Current implementation\n- `contracts/proxies/ShowPassProxy.sol` - Proxy contract with timelock\n- `docs/contracts/UPGRADEABILITY_GUIDE.md` - Upgrade workflow documentation\n- `docs/contracts/CONTRACT_INTERACTIONS.md` - ABI and interaction patterns\n- `packages/backend/src/infrastructure/adapters/blockchain/` - Off-chain adapters\n- OpenZeppelin UUPS Documentation: https://docs.openzeppelin.com/contracts/4.x/api/proxy#UUPSUpgradeable\n\n---\n\n## 8. Conclusion\n\nThe addition of a tiered royalty system to `ShowPass.sol` is a moderate-complexity upgrade that requires careful coordination between on-chain and off-chain components. The UUPS proxy pattern provides flexibility, but demands strict adherence to storage layout rules.\n\n**Key Takeaways:**\n1. The upgrade mechanism is **UUPS** (not Transparent Proxy as some docs suggest)\n2. The backend has **three critical ABI-dependent files** that must be updated: `ethers.service.ts`, `contract.mapper.ts`, and `stage-event.observer.ts`\n3. The upgrade sequence must follow a **strict on-chain-first, off-chain-second** order\n4. **Storage collision** is the highest-risk issue and requires tooling-assisted validation\n5. A **48-hour timelock** provides a natural review window for governance\n\nThe recommended approach is to treat this as a coordinated release across all platform components, with comprehensive testing on Mumbai/Goerli testnet before mainnet deployment.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
